import re
import math
from pathlib import Path

from common.core.models_base import (
    Footprint, Pad, PadShape, PadType, Line, Circle, Arc, Text, TextType,
    default_layers_for_pad_type,
)
from kicad.units import kicad_to_mm
from kicad.common_layers import (
    KICAD_LAYER_TO_INTERNAL, LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL, layer_from_id,
)


TEXT_PATTERN_SIMPLE = re.compile(
    r'^T(\d+)\s+([^\r\n"]+?)\s+"([^"]*)"',
    re.MULTILINE
)


def parse_kicad_mod(filepath):
    content = Path(filepath).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
    return parse_kicad_content(content)


def parse_kicad_content(content):
    footprints = []
    pattern = re.compile(r'\$MODULE\s+(\S+)(.*?)\$EndMODULE', re.DOTALL)
    for match in pattern.finditer(content):
        name = match.group(1)
        body = match.group(2)
        fp = parse_module(name, body)
        footprints.append(fp)
    return footprints


def parse_module(name, body):
    fp = Footprint(name=name)

    t0_match = re.search(r'^T0\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    t1_match = re.search(r'^T1\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)

    if t0_match:
        ref_text = parse_text_line(t0_match.group(1), TextType.REFERENCE)
        ref_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(ref_text)
        fp.reference = ref_text.text

    if t1_match:
        val_text = parse_text_line(t1_match.group(1), TextType.VALUE)
        val_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(val_text)
        fp.value = val_text.text

    for text_match in TEXT_PATTERN_SIMPLE.finditer(body):
        idx = int(text_match.group(1))
        if idx in (0, 1):
            continue
        params = text_match.group(2).split()
        text_value = text_match.group(3)

        x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
        y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
        w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
        h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
        size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0

        visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]

        layer = "F.SilkS"
        for tok in params:
            try:
                layer_id = int(tok)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
                layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
                break

        t = Text(
            x=x, y=y,
            text=text_value if text_value else "Text",
            size=size,
            visible=visible,
            text_type=TextType.NORMAL,
            layer=layer,
        )
        fp.texts.append(t)

    for ds_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DS\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness, layer_id = ds_match.groups()
        line = Line(
            x=kicad_to_mm(x1), y=kicad_to_mm(y1),
            x2=kicad_to_mm(x2), y2=kicad_to_mm(y2),
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        )
        fp.lines.append(line)

    for dc_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DC\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        xc, yc, xp, yp, thickness, layer_id = dc_match.groups()
        xc_mm = kicad_to_mm(xc)
        yc_mm = kicad_to_mm(yc)
        xp_mm = kicad_to_mm(xp)
        yp_mm = kicad_to_mm(yp)
        radius = math.hypot(xp_mm - xc_mm, yp_mm - yc_mm)
        circle = Circle(
            x=xc_mm, y=yc_mm,
            radius=radius,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        )
        fp.circles.append(circle)

    for da_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DA\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        cx, cy, sx, sy, angle, thickness, layer_id = da_match.groups()
        arc = Arc(
            x=kicad_to_mm(cx), y=kicad_to_mm(cy),
            sx=kicad_to_mm(sx), sy=kicad_to_mm(sy),
            angle=float(angle) / 10.0,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        )
        fp.arcs.append(arc)

    pad_pattern = re.compile(r'\$PAD(.*?)\$EndPAD', re.DOTALL)
    for pad_match in pad_pattern.finditer(body):
        pad = parse_pad(pad_match.group(1))
        if pad:
            fp.pads.append(pad)

    return fp


def parse_text_line(text_str, text_type):
    text_match = re.search(r'"([^"]*)"', text_str)
    text = text_match.group(1) if text_match else ""

    params_str = text_str[:text_match.start()].strip() if text_match else text_str
    params = params_str.split()

    x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
    y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
    w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
    h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
    size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0

    visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]

    layer = "F.SilkS"
    for tok in params:
        try:
            layer_id = int(tok)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
            layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
            break

    return Text(
        x=x, y=y, text=text, size=size,
        visible=visible, text_type=text_type, locked=True,
        layer=layer,
    )


def parse_pad(pad_body):
    sh_match = re.search(r'Sh\s+"([^"]*)"\s+(\w+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    dr_match = re.search(r'Dr\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    po_match = re.search(r'Po\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)', pad_body)
    at_match = re.search(r'At\s+(\w+)', pad_body)
    la_match = re.search(r'^La\s+([^\r\n]+)', pad_body, re.MULTILINE)

    if not sh_match or not po_match:
        return None

    number = sh_match.group(1)
    shape_str = sh_match.group(2)
    size_x = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(3))
    size_y = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(4))
    x = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(1))
    y = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(2))
    drill = kicad_to_mm(dr_match.group(1)) if dr_match else 0

    shape_map = {'R': PadShape.RECT, 'C': PadShape.CIRCLE, 'O': PadShape.OVAL}
    shape = shape_map.get(shape_str, PadShape.CIRCLE)

    pad_type = PadType.STANDARD
    if at_match:
        attr = at_match.group(1)
        if attr == 'SMD':
            pad_type = PadType.SMD
        elif attr == 'CONN':
            pad_type = PadType.CONN
        else:
            pad_type = PadType.STANDARD

    layers = []
    if la_match:
        for token in la_match.group(1).split():
            internal = KICAD_LAYER_TO_INTERNAL.get(token)
            if internal and internal not in layers:
                layers.append(internal)

    if not layers:
        layers = default_layers_for_pad_type(pad_type)

    return Pad(
        x=x, y=y,
        number=number, shape=shape, pad_type=pad_type,
        width=size_x, height=size_y,
        drill=drill if drill > 0 else None,
        layers=layers,
    ).
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\core\autoplacer.py

import math
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from multiprocessing import Pool

from common.core.models_base import TextType
from pbe_editor.core import sequential_algorithm as algo


class Metric(Enum):
    EUCLIDEAN = "euclidean"
    MANHATTAN = "manhattan"


class DirectiveMode(Enum):
    LARGEST = "largest"
    MOST_CONNECTED = "most_connected"


class DirectivePosition(Enum):
    CENTER = "center"
    TOP_LEFT = "top_left"
    BOTTOM_LEFT = "bottom_left"


class JMethod(Enum):
    M_331 = "3.3.1"
    M_333 = "3.3.3"
    M_335 = "3.3.5"
    M_336 = "3.3.6"


class OptimizationMode(Enum):
    NONE = "none"
    D_C = "c"
    D_C2 = "c2"
    D_C4 = "c4"


@dataclass
class PlacementParams:
    A: float = 2.0
    B: float = 2.0
    C: float = 1.25
    metric: Metric = Metric.EUCLIDEAN
    j_method: JMethod = JMethod.M_335
    directive_mode: DirectiveMode = DirectiveMode.LARGEST
    directive_position: DirectivePosition = DirectivePosition.CENTER
    threads: int = 1
    optimization_mode: OptimizationMode = OptimizationMode.D_C2


@dataclass
class PlacementResult:
    positions: dict[int, tuple[float, float]]
    time_seconds: float
    num_footprints: int
    num_connections: int
    wire_euclidean: float
    wire_manhattan: float
    directive_ref: str = ""
    threads_used: int = 1
    board_width: float = 0.0
    board_length: float = 0.0
    seq_wire_euclidean: float = 0.0
    seq_wire_manhattan: float = 0.0
    seq_time_seconds: float = 0.0
    opt_time_seconds: float = 0.0


def _J_task(args):
    c, c_331, jm, i, placed, unplaced, n = args
    return i, algo.calc_J(c, c_331, jm, i, placed, unplaced, n)


def _F_task(args):
    conn_row, candidates, offset, placed_positions, metric_name = args
    ox, oy = offset
    return [
        (gx, gy, algo.pick_best_position((gx + ox, gy + oy), placed_positions, metric_name))
        for gx, gy in candidates
    ]


def _arc_points(cx, cy, sx, sy, angle_deg, segments=16):
    r = math.hypot(sx - cx, sy - cy)
    if r < 1e-9:
        return [(cx, cy), (sx, sy)]
    a1 = math.atan2(sy - cy, sx - cx)
    sweep = math.radians(angle_deg)
    pts = []
    for i in range(segments + 1):
        t = i / segments
        a = a1 + t * sweep
        pts.append((cx + r * math.cos(a), cy + r * math.sin(a)))
    return pts


def _rotate_offset(dx, dy, angle_deg):
    if not angle_deg:
        return (dx, dy)
    rad = math.radians(angle_deg)
    cos_a = math.cos(rad)
    sin_a = math.sin(rad)
    return (dx * cos_a - dy * sin_a, dx * sin_a + dy * cos_a)


def _get_local_bbox(module):
    fp = module.footprint
    xs, ys = [], []
    for pad in fp.pads:
        xs.extend([pad.x - pad.width / 2, pad.x + pad.width / 2])
        ys.extend([pad.y - pad.height / 2, pad.y + pad.height / 2])
    for line in fp.lines:
        xs.extend([line.x, line.x2])
        ys.extend([line.y, line.y2])
    for circle in fp.circles:
        xs.extend([circle.x - circle.radius, circle.x + circle.radius])
        ys.extend([circle.y - circle.radius, circle.y + circle.radius])
    for arc in fp.arcs:
        for px, py in _arc_points(arc.x, arc.y, arc.sx, arc.sy, arc.angle, 16):
            xs.append(px)
            ys.append(py)
    for text in fp.texts:
        if getattr(text, 'text_type', None) in (TextType.REFERENCE, TextType.VALUE, 0):
            continue
        xs.append(text.x)
        ys.append(text.y)
    if not xs:
        return (-1.0, -1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
    return (min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys))


def _mst_length(points, metric):
    n = len(points)
    if n < 2:
        return 0.0
    in_tree = [False] * n
    min_d = [float('inf')] * n
    min_d[0] = 0.0
    total = 0.0
    for _ in range(n):
        best, best_d = -1, float('inf')
        for i in range(n):
            if not in_tree[i] and min_d[i] < best_d:
                best_d, best = min_d[i], i
        if best == -1:
            break
        in_tree[best] = True
        total += best_d
        bx, by = points[best]
        for i in range(n):
            if in_tree[i]:
                continue
            ix, iy = points[i]
            d = abs(ix - bx) + abs(iy - by) if metric == Metric.MANHATTAN else math.hypot(ix - bx, iy - by)
            if d < min_d[i]:
                min_d[i] = d
    return total


def _get_board_edge_rect(board):
    xs, ys = [], []
    for line in board.lines:
        if line.layer == "Edge.Cuts":
            xs.extend([line.x1, line.x2])
            ys.extend([line.y1, line.y2])
    if not xs:
        raise ValueError("На плате отсутствует контур Edge.Cuts. Нарисуйте границы платы.")
    if max(xs) - min(xs) <= 0 or max(ys) - min(ys) <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Границы платы нулевого размера.")
    return (min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys))


class _BoardSnapshot:
    def __init__(self, board):
        self.edge_rect = _get_board_edge_rect(board)
        self.modules = []
        for m in board.modules:
            bbox = _get_local_bbox(m)
            self.modules.append({
                'uid': m.uid,
                'reference': m.reference,
                'x': m.x,
                'y': m.y,
                'angle': m.angle,
                'pad_nets': dict(m.pad_nets),
                'pads': {pad.number: (pad.x, pad.y) for pad in m.footprint.pads},
                'bbox': bbox,
                'center_offset': ((bbox[0] + bbox[2]) / 2.0, (bbox[1] + bbox[3]) / 2.0),
            })


class SequentialPlacer:
    def __init__(self, snapshot, params):
        self.snap = snapshot
        self.p = params
        self.n = len(snapshot.modules)

        self.edge_rect = snapshot.edge_rect
        self.inner_rect = (self.edge_rect[0] + params.A, self.edge_rect[1] + params.A,
                           self.edge_rect[2] - params.A, self.edge_rect[3] - params.A)

        if self.inner_rect[0] >= self.inner_rect[2] or self.inner_rect[1] >= self.inner_rect[3]:
            raise ValueError("Параметр A слишком велик для данной платы.")

        self.bboxes = [m['bbox'] for m in snapshot.modules]
        self.center_offsets = [m['center_offset'] for m in snapshot.modules]
        self.positions = {}
        self.placed = set()
        self.unplaced = set(range(self.n))

        self.c, self.c_331 = algo.compute_connectivity_matrix(snapshot)

        area = (self.inner_rect[2] - self.inner_rect[0]) * (self.inner_rect[3] - self.inner_rect[1])
        cell_count = max(1.0, area / (params.C * params.C))
        self._work_estimate = cell_count * self.n * self.n

        self._pool = None
        self.threads_used = 1
        if params.threads > 1 and self._parallel_worthwhile():
            try:
                self._pool = Pool(processes=params.threads)
                self.threads_used = params.threads
            except Exception:
                self._pool = None
                self.threads_used = 1

    def _parallel_worthwhile(self):
        return self._work_estimate >= 5_000_000

    def _shutdown_pool(self):
        if self._pool is not None:
            self._pool.terminate()
            self._pool.join()
            self._pool = None

    def _pick_directive(self):
        mode = "largest" if self.p.directive_mode == DirectiveMode.LARGEST else "most_connected"
        return algo.pick_directive_element(self.bboxes, self.c, mode, self.p.directive_position.value)

    def _get_directive_pos(self, i):
        lb = self.bboxes[i]
        if self.p.directive_position == DirectivePosition.CENTER:
            cx = (self.edge_rect[0] + self.edge_rect[2]) / 2.0
            cy = (self.edge_rect[1] + self.edge_rect[3]) / 2.0
            return (cx - (lb[0] + lb[2]) / 2, cy - (lb[1] + lb[3]) / 2)
        elif self.p.directive_position == DirectivePosition.TOP_LEFT:
            ix = self.inner_rect[0]
            iy = self.inner_rect[1]
            return (ix - lb[0], iy - lb[1])
        else:
            ix = self.inner_rect[0]
            iy = self.inner_rect[3]
            return (ix - lb[0], iy - lb[3])

    def _snap_to_grid(self, i, gx, gy):
        lb = self.bboxes[i]
        g = self.p.C
        ix_lo = math.ceil((self.inner_rect[0] - lb[0]) / g - 1e-9)
        ix_hi = math.floor((self.inner_rect[2] - lb[2]) / g + 1e-9)
        iy_lo = math.ceil((self.inner_rect[1] - lb[1]) / g - 1e-9)
        iy_hi = math.floor((self.inner_rect[3] - lb[3]) / g + 1e-9)
        if ix_lo > ix_hi or iy_lo > iy_hi:
            return None
        ix = max(ix_lo, min(ix_hi, round(gx / g)))
        iy = max(iy_lo, min(iy_hi, round(gy / g)))
        return (ix * g, iy * g)

    def _get_box(self, i, gx, gy):
        lb = self.bboxes[i]
        return (gx + lb[0], gy + lb[1], gx + lb[2], gy + lb[3])

    def _collides(self, box, exclude):
        for j in self.placed:
            if j == exclude:
                continue
            ob = self._get_box(j, *self.positions[j])
            if (box[0] < ob[2] + self.p.B and box[2] > ob[0] - self.p.B and
                    box[1] < ob[3] + self.p.B and box[3] > ob[1] - self.p.B):
                return True
        return False

    def _within_board(self, box):
        return (box[0] >= self.inner_rect[0] - 1e-9 and box[1] >= self.inner_rect[1] - 1e-9 and
                box[2] <= self.inner_rect[2] + 1e-9 and box[3] <= self.inner_rect[3] + 1e-9)

    def _get_candidates(self, i):
        if not self.placed:
            pos = self._snap_to_grid(i, *self._get_directive_pos(i))
            return [pos] if pos else []
        g = self.p.C
        candidates = []
        ix_range = range(int(math.ceil(self.inner_rect[0] / g)), int(math.floor(self.inner_rect[2] / g)) + 1)
        iy_range = range(int(math.ceil(self.inner_rect[1] / g)), int(math.floor(self.inner_rect[3] / g)) + 1)
        for ix in ix_range:
            for iy in iy_range:
                gx, gy = ix * g, iy * g
                box = self._get_box(i, gx, gy)
                if self._within_board(box) and not self._collides(box, i):
                    candidates.append((gx, gy))
        return candidates

    def _get_placed_positions(self, conn_row):
        result = []
        for j in self.placed:
            px, py = self.positions[j]
            ox, oy = self.center_offsets[j]
            result.append((px + ox, py + oy, conn_row[j]))
        return result

    def _compute_J_scores(self):
        items = list(self.unplaced)
        if not items:
            return {}
        jm = self.p.j_method.value
        if self._pool is None or len(items) < 4:
            return {i: algo.calc_J(self.c, self.c_331, jm, i, self.placed, self.unplaced, self.n)
                    for i in items}
        jobs = [(self.c, self.c_331, jm, i, self.placed, self.unplaced, self.n) for i in items]
        chunk = max(1, len(jobs) // (self.p.threads * 4))
        results = self._pool.map(_J_task, jobs, chunksize=chunk)
        return dict(results)

    def _compute_F_scores(self, best_i, cands):
        if not cands:
            return []
        conn = self.c_331 if self.p.j_method == JMethod.M_331 else self.c
        conn_row = conn[best_i]
        placed_positions = self._get_placed_positions(conn_row)
        metric_name = "manhattan" if self.p.metric == Metric.MANHATTAN else "euclidean"
        offset = self.center_offsets[best_i]
        ox, oy = offset
        work = len(cands) * max(1, len(placed_positions))
        if self._pool is None or work < 2000:
            return [
                ((gx, gy), algo.pick_best_position((gx + ox, gy + oy), placed_positions, metric_name))
                for gx, gy in cands
            ]
        n_chunks = self.p.threads * 4
        chunk_size = max(1, (len(cands) + n_chunks - 1) // n_chunks)
        chunks = [cands[k:k + chunk_size] for k in range(0, len(cands), chunk_size)]
        jobs = [(conn_row, ch, offset, placed_positions, metric_name) for ch in chunks]
        results = self._pool.map(_F_task, jobs, chunksize=1)
        out = []
        for ch in results:
            for gx, gy, v in ch:
                out.append(((gx, gy), v))
        return out

    def _get_pad_abs(self, i, pad_num):
        mod = self.snap.modules[i]
        lx, ly = mod['pads'][pad_num]
        if i in self.positions:
            gx, gy = self.positions[i]
            return (gx + lx, gy + ly)
        rx, ry = _rotate_offset(lx, ly, mod.get('angle', 0.0))
        return (mod['x'] + rx, mod['y'] + ry)

    def _calc_total_wire(self, metric):
        total = 0.0
        for pins in self._get_net_pins().values():
            if len(pins) >= 2:
                pts = [self._get_pad_abs(i, pad_num) for _, pad_num, i in pins]
                total += _mst_length(pts, metric)
        return total

    def _get_net_pins(self):
        nets = {}
        for i, mod in enumerate(self.snap.modules):
            pads = mod['pads']
            for pad_num, pad_net in mod['pad_nets'].items():
                if pad_net.net_code <= 0 or not pad_net.net_name:
                    continue
                if pad_num not in pads:
                    continue
                nets.setdefault(pad_net.net_code, []).append((mod['reference'], pad_num, i))
        for code in nets:
            nets[code].sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
        return nets

    def _opt_step(self):
        if self.p.optimization_mode == OptimizationMode.D_C:
            return self.p.C
        if self.p.optimization_mode == OptimizationMode.D_C2:
            return self.p.C / 2.0
        if self.p.optimization_mode == OptimizationMode.D_C4:
            return self.p.C / 4.0
        return 0.0

    def _run_extra_optimization(self):
        d = self._opt_step()
        if d <= 0:
            return
        net_pins = self._get_net_pins()
        active_nets = {code: pins for code, pins in net_pins.items() if len(pins) >= 2}
        if not active_nets:
            return

        mod_nets = [[] for _ in range(self.n)]
        net_lengths = {}
        for code, pins in active_nets.items():
            pts = [self._get_pad_abs(m_idx, pad_num) for _, pad_num, m_idx in pins]
            net_lengths[code] = _mst_length(pts, self.p.metric)
            seen = set()
            for _, _, m_idx in pins:
                if m_idx not in seen:
                    seen.add(m_idx)
                    mod_nets[m_idx].append(code)

        directions = ((d, 0.0), (-d, 0.0), (0.0, d), (0.0, -d))
        for _ in range(500):
            improved = False
            for i in range(self.n):
                incident = mod_nets[i]
                if not incident:
                    continue
                cur_x, cur_y = self.positions[i]
                cur_sub = sum(net_lengths[code] for code in incident)

                for dx, dy in directions:
                    while True:
                        nx = round((cur_x + dx) / d) * d
                        ny = round((cur_y + dy) / d) * d
                        box = self._get_box(i, nx, ny)
                        if not self._within_board(box) or self._collides(box, i):
                            break
                        self.positions[i] = (nx, ny)
                        cand_lens = {}
                        cand_sub = 0.0
                        for code in incident:
                            pts = [self._get_pad_abs(m_idx, pad_num) for _, pad_num, m_idx in active_nets[code]]
                            nl = _mst_length(pts, self.p.metric)
                            cand_lens[code] = nl
                            cand_sub += nl
                        if cand_sub < cur_sub - 1e-9:
                            cur_x, cur_y = nx, ny
                            cur_sub = cand_sub
                            for code, nl in cand_lens.items():
                                net_lengths[code] = nl
                            improved = True
                        else:
                            self.positions[i] = (cur_x, cur_y)
                            break

            if not improved:
                break

    def run(self):
        board_width = self.edge_rect[2] - self.edge_rect[0]
        board_length = self.edge_rect[3] - self.edge_rect[1]
        if self.n == 0:
            return PlacementResult(
                {}, 0.0, 0, 0, 0.0, 0.0, "", self.threads_used,
                board_width, board_length, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0,
            )
        try:
            seq_start = time.perf_counter()
            idx = self._pick_directive()
            pos = self._snap_to_grid(idx, *self._get_directive_pos(idx))
            if pos is None:
                ref = self.snap.modules[idx]['reference']
                raise RuntimeError(f"Корпус {ref} не помещается на плату.")
            self.positions[idx] = pos
            self.placed.add(idx)
            self.unplaced.discard(idx)

            while self.unplaced:
                j_scores = self._compute_J_scores()
                best_i = max(j_scores, key=lambda x: (j_scores[x], -x))

                cands = self._get_candidates(best_i)
                if not cands:
                    ref = self.snap.modules[best_i]['reference']
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Корпус {ref} не помещается на плату с заданными зазорами.")

                f_scores = self._compute_F_scores(best_i, cands)
                best_pos = min(f_scores, key=lambda x: (x[1], x[0][1], x[0][0]))[0]

                self.positions[best_i] = best_pos
                self.placed.add(best_i)
                self.unplaced.discard(best_i)

            seq_duration = time.perf_counter() - seq_start

            seq_w_euc = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.EUCLIDEAN)
            seq_w_man = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.MANHATTAN)

            if self.p.optimization_mode != OptimizationMode.NONE:
                opt_start = time.perf_counter()
                self._run_extra_optimization()
                opt_duration = time.perf_counter() - opt_start
                w_euc = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.EUCLIDEAN)
                w_man = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.MANHATTAN)
            else:
                opt_duration = 0.0
                w_euc = seq_w_euc
                w_man = seq_w_man
        finally:
            self._shutdown_pool()

        total_duration = seq_duration + opt_duration
        conn_count = sum(len(v) - 1 for v in self._get_net_pins().values() if len(v) >= 2)

        return PlacementResult(
            self.positions, total_duration, self.n, conn_count, w_euc, w_man,
            self.snap.modules[idx]['reference'], self.threads_used,
            board_width, board_length, seq_w_euc, seq_w_man,
            seq_duration, opt_duration,
        )


def _snapshot_wire_lengths(snapshot):
    placer = SequentialPlacer.__new__(SequentialPlacer)
    placer.snap = snapshot
    placer.n = len(snapshot.modules)
    placer.positions = {}
    placer.placed = set()
    placer.unplaced = set(range(placer.n))
    return placer._calc_total_wire(Metric.EUCLIDEAN), placer._calc_total_wire(Metric.MANHATTAN)


def run_auto_placement(board, params, threads=1):
    snapshot = _BoardSnapshot(board)
    before_euc, before_man = _snapshot_wire_lengths(snapshot)

    params.threads = threads
    result = SequentialPlacer(snapshot, params).run()

    return result, before_euc, before_man.

from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF

from pbe_editor.core.board_models import is_gnd_net, is_power_net

class BoardManager:
    def __init__(self, board, scene, canvas):
        self.board = board
        self.scene = scene
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.items = {}
        self.line_items = {}
        self.circle_items = {}
        self.arc_items = {}
        self.text_items = {}
        self.ratsnest_items = []
        self.show_ratsnest = True

    def add_module(self, module):
        item = BoardModuleGraphicsItem(module, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.items[module.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_line(self, line):
        item = BoardLineGraphicsItem(line, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.line_items[line.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_circle(self, circle):
        item = BoardCircleGraphicsItem(circle, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.circle_items[circle.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_arc(self, arc):
        item = BoardArcGraphicsItem(arc, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.arc_items[arc.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_text(self, text):
        item = BoardTextGraphicsItem(text, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.text_items[text.uid] = item
        return item

    def remove_module(self, module):
        item = self.items.pop(module.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_line(self, line):
        item = self.line_items.pop(line.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_circle(self, circle):
        item = self.circle_items.pop(circle.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_arc(self, arc):
        item = self.arc_items.pop(arc.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_text(self, text):
        item = self.text_items.pop(text.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def clear(self):
        all_items = (list(self.items.values()) + list(self.line_items.values())
                     + list(self.circle_items.values()) + list(self.arc_items.values())
                     + list(self.text_items.values()))
        for item in all_items:
            try:
                if item.scene() is self.scene:
                    self.scene.removeItem(item)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        self.items.clear()
        self.line_items.clear()
        self.circle_items.clear()
        self.arc_items.clear()
        self.text_items.clear()
        self.clear_ratsnest()

    def clear_ratsnest(self):
        for item in self.ratsnest_items:
            try:
                if item.scene() is self.scene:
                    self.scene.removeItem(item)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        self.ratsnest_items.clear()

    def rebuild_ratsnest(self):
        self.clear_ratsnest()
        if not self.show_ratsnest:
            return
        net_pins = self.board.collect_net_pins()
        for code, pins in net_pins.items():
            if len(pins) < 2:
                continue
            net_name = pins[0][0].pad_nets.get(pins[0][1])
            net_name = net_name.net_name if net_name else ""
            is_gnd = is_gnd_net(net_name)
            is_power = is_power_net(net_name) and not is_gnd
            points = [p[2] for p in pins]
            edges = build_mst_edges(points)
            for a_idx, b_idx in edges:
                module_a, pad_a, pos_a = pins[a_idx]
                module_b, pad_b, pos_b = pins[b_idx]
                item = RatsnestGraphicsItem(
                    QPointF(*pos_a), QPointF(*pos_b),
                    net_code=code, net_name=net_name,
                    is_power=is_power, is_gnd=is_gnd,
                    label_a=f"{module_a.reference}.{pad_a}",
                    label_b=f"{module_b.reference}.{pad_b}",
                )
                self.scene.addItem(item)
                self.ratsnest_items.append(item)

    def refresh_ratsnest_for_module(self, module):
        self.rebuild_ratsnest().

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import copy
import math
import re

from pbe_editor.core.models import Footprint


POWER_NET_NAMES = {"GND", "VCC", "VDD", "VSS", "+5V", "+3V3", "+3.3V", "-5V", "-12V", "+12V"}
GND_NET_NAMES = {"GND"}


def is_power_net(name: str) -> bool:
    if not name:
        return False
    return name.upper() in {n.upper() for n in POWER_NET_NAMES}


def is_gnd_net(name: str) -> bool:
    if not name:
        return False
    return name.upper() in {n.upper() for n in GND_NET_NAMES}


_PREFIX_HINTS = [
    (re.compile(r"^diode", re.IGNORECASE), "VD"),
    (re.compile(r"^d_bridge", re.IGNORECASE), "VD"),
    (re.compile(r"^tran", re.IGNORECASE), "VT"),
    (re.compile(r"^cap", re.IGNORECASE), "C"),
    (re.compile(r"^res|^r$", re.IGNORECASE), "R"),
    (re.compile(r"^con", re.IGNORECASE), "X"),
    (re.compile(r"^7seg", re.IGNORECASE), "HG"),
    (re.compile(r"^k\d", re.IGNORECASE), "DD"),
    (re.compile(r"^led", re.IGNORECASE), "LED"),
]


def prefix_from_footprint_name(name: str) -> str:
    for rx, prefix in _PREFIX_HINTS:
        if rx.search(name or ""):
            return prefix
    letters = re.findall(r"[A-Za-zА-Яа-я]+", name or "")
    return (letters[0][:3] if letters else "U").upper()


def _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo):
    cls = self.__class__
    result = cls.__new__(cls)
    memo[id(self)] = result
    for k, v in self.__dict__.items():
        if k == 'graphics_item':
            setattr(result, k, None)
        else:
            setattr(result, k, copy.deepcopy(v, memo))
    return result


@dataclass
class PadNet:
    pad_number: str
    net_code: int
    net_name: str


@dataclass
class Net:
    code: int
    name: str


@dataclass
class BoardModule:
    footprint: Footprint
    reference: str
    value: str
    x: float = 0.0
    y: float = 0.0
    angle: float = 0.0
    layer: str = "F.Cu"
    pad_nets: Dict[str, PadNet] = field(default_factory=dict)
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)

    def get_pad_absolute(self, pad_number: str):
        pad = None
        for p in self.footprint.pads:
            if p.number == pad_number:
                pad = p
                break
        if pad is None:
            return None
        rad = math.radians(self.angle)
        cos_a = math.cos(rad)
        sin_a = math.sin(rad)
        lx = pad.x
        ly = pad.y
        gx = self.x + lx * cos_a - ly * sin_a
        gy = self.y + lx * sin_a + ly * cos_a
        return gx, gy

    def get_footprint_copy(self) -> Footprint:
        return copy.deepcopy(self.footprint)


@dataclass
class BoardLine:
    x1: float
    y1: float
    x2: float
    y2: float
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    width: float = 0.381
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class BoardCircle:
    x: float
    y: float
    radius: float
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    width: float = 0.381
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class BoardArc:
    x: float
    y: float
    sx: float
    sy: float
    angle: float = 90.0
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    width: float = 0.381
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class BoardText:
    x: float
    y: float
    text: str = "Text"
    size: float = 1.0
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    visible: bool = True
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class Board:
    name: str = "Untitled"
    modules: List[BoardModule] = field(default_factory=list)
    lines: List[BoardLine] = field(default_factory=list)
    circles: List[BoardCircle] = field(default_factory=list)
    arcs: List[BoardArc] = field(default_factory=list)
    texts: List[BoardText] = field(default_factory=list)
    nets: Dict[int, Net] = field(default_factory=dict)
    filename: Optional[str] = None

    def get_net_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Net]:
        for net in self.nets.values():
            if net.name == name:
                return net
        return None

    def get_or_create_net(self, name: str) -> Net:
        net = self.get_net_by_name(name)
        if net is not None:
            return net
        code = max(self.nets.keys()) + 1 if self.nets else 1
        net = Net(code=code, name=name)
        self.nets[code] = net
        return net

    def get_net_by_code(self, code: int) -> Optional[Net]:
        return self.nets.get(code)

    def collect_net_pins(self):
        result = {}
        for module in self.modules:
            for pad_number, pad_net in module.pad_nets.items():
                if pad_net.net_code <= 0:
                    continue
                if not pad_net.net_name:
                    continue
                pos = module.get_pad_absolute(pad_number)
                if pos is None:
                    continue
                result.setdefault(pad_net.net_code, []).append((module, pad_number, pos))
        for code in result:
            result[code].sort(key=lambda x: (x[0].reference, x[1]))
        return result

    def auto_reference(self, prefix: str = "U") -> str:
        used = set()
        for m in self.modules:
            ref = m.reference or ""
            mm = re.match(r"^([A-Za-zА-Яа-я]+)(\d+)$", ref)
            if mm and mm.group(1).upper() == prefix.upper():
                used.add(int(mm.group(2)))
        i = 1
        while i in used:
            i += 1
        return f"{prefix}{i}"

    def find_pad_at(self, scene_x: float, scene_y: float, tolerance: float):
        best = None
        best_dist = tolerance
        for module in self.modules:
            for pad in module.footprint.pads:
                pos = module.get_pad_absolute(pad.number)
                if pos is None:
                    continue
                dx = scene_x - pos[0]
                dy = scene_y - pos[1]
                dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
                if dist <= best_dist:
                    best_dist = dist
                    best = (module, pad.number)
        return best.
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\core\netlist_parser.py
============================================================
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List


@dataclass
class NetlistComponent:
    timestamp: str
    footprint: str
    reference: str
    value: str
    pins: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)


def parse_netlist(filepath: str) -> List[NetlistComponent]:
    content = Path(filepath).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
    return parse_netlist_content(content)


def parse_netlist_content(content: str) -> List[NetlistComponent]:
    components: List[NetlistComponent] = []
    lines = content.splitlines()
    i = 0
    n = len(lines)
    while i < n:
        line = lines[i].strip()
        if not line.startswith('( /'):
            i += 1
            continue

        match = re.match(
            r'^\(\s+(/\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+(.*?)\s*$',
            line
        )
        if not match:
            i += 1
            continue

        timestamp = match.group(1)
        footprint = match.group(2)
        reference = match.group(3)
        value = match.group(4).strip()

        component = NetlistComponent(
            timestamp=timestamp,
            footprint=footprint,
            reference=reference,
            value=value,
        )

        i += 1
        while i < n:
            pin_line = lines[i].strip()
            if pin_line == ')':
                break
            pin_match = re.match(r'^\(\s*(\S+)\s+(.+?)\s*\)\s*$', pin_line)
            if pin_match:
                pin_number = pin_match.group(1)
                net_name = pin_match.group(2).strip().strip('"')
                component.pins[pin_number] = net_name
            i += 1

        components.append(component)
        i += 1

    return components.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\core\pairwise_placer.py

import math
import random
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum

from common.core.models_base import TextType
from pbe_editor.core import pairwise_algorithm as algo
from pbe_editor.core.autoplacer import (
    Metric,
    OptimizationMode,
    _BoardSnapshot,
    _mst_length,
    _rotate_offset,
    _snapshot_wire_lengths,
)


class PairwiseAlgoVersion(Enum):
    SAPRYKIN = "saprykin"
    SELYUTIN = "selyutin"


class PairwiseDirectiveMode(Enum):
    NONE = "none"
    LARGEST = "largest"
    MOST_CONNECTED = "most_connected"


@dataclass
class PairwiseParams:
    A: float = 2.0
    B: float = 2.0
    C: float = 1.25
    algo_version: PairwiseAlgoVersion = PairwiseAlgoVersion.SAPRYKIN
    metric: Metric = Metric.EUCLIDEAN
    directive_mode: PairwiseDirectiveMode = PairwiseDirectiveMode.NONE
    optimization_mode: OptimizationMode = OptimizationMode.D_C2


@dataclass
class PairwiseResult:
    positions: dict[int, tuple[float, float]]
    time_seconds: float
    num_footprints: int
    num_connections: int
    wire_euclidean: float
    wire_manhattan: float
    directive_ref: str = ""
    board_width: float = 0.0
    board_length: float = 0.0
    pair_wire_euclidean: float = 0.0
    pair_wire_manhattan: float = 0.0
    pair_time_seconds: float = 0.0
    opt_time_seconds: float = 0.0
    shifted_count: int = 0


class PairwisePlacer:
    def __init__(self, snapshot, params):
        self.snap = snapshot
        self.p = params
        self.n = len(snapshot.modules)

        self.edge_rect = snapshot.edge_rect
        self.inner_rect = (
            self.edge_rect[0] + params.A,
            self.edge_rect[1] + params.A,
            self.edge_rect[2] - params.A,
            self.edge_rect[3] - params.A,
        )

        if self.inner_rect[0] >= self.inner_rect[2] or self.inner_rect[1] >= self.inner_rect[3]:
            raise ValueError("Параметр A слишком велик для данной платы.")

        self.bboxes = [m['bbox'] for m in snapshot.modules]
        self.center_offsets = [m['center_offset'] for m in snapshot.modules]
        self.positions = {}
        self.shifted_count = 0

        self.c = algo.calc_connectivity_matrix(snapshot)

    def _get_box(self, i, gx, gy):
        lb = self.bboxes[i]
        return (gx + lb[0], gy + lb[1], gx + lb[2], gy + lb[3])

    def _within_board(self, box):
        return (
            box[0] >= self.inner_rect[0] - 1e-9
            and box[1] >= self.inner_rect[1] - 1e-9
            and box[2] <= self.inner_rect[2] + 1e-9
            and box[3] <= self.inner_rect[3] + 1e-9
        )

    def _collides_with(self, box, placed_dict, exclude=-1):
        for j, (px, py) in placed_dict.items():
            if j == exclude:
                continue
            ob = self._get_box(j, px, py)
            if (
                box[0] < ob[2] + self.p.B - 1e-9
                and box[2] > ob[0] - self.p.B + 1e-9
                and box[1] < ob[3] + self.p.B - 1e-9
                and box[3] > ob[1] - self.p.B + 1e-9
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _clamp_to_inner(self, i, gx, gy):
        lb = self.bboxes[i]
        min_gx = self.inner_rect[0] - lb[0]
        max_gx = self.inner_rect[2] - lb[2]
        min_gy = self.inner_rect[1] - lb[1]
        max_gy = self.inner_rect[3] - lb[3]
        if min_gx > max_gx + 1e-9 or min_gy > max_gy + 1e-9:
            ref = self.snap.modules[i]['reference']
            raise RuntimeError(f"Корпус {ref} не помещается на плату с заданным отступом A.")
        nx = max(min_gx, min(max_gx, gx))
        ny = max(min_gy, min(max_gy, gy))
        return (nx, ny)

    def _snap_near(self, i, gx, gy):
        lb = self.bboxes[i]
        g = self.p.C
        ix_lo = math.ceil((self.inner_rect[0] - lb[0]) / g - 1e-9)
        ix_hi = math.floor((self.inner_rect[2] - lb[2]) / g + 1e-9)
        iy_lo = math.ceil((self.inner_rect[1] - lb[1]) / g - 1e-9)
        iy_hi = math.floor((self.inner_rect[3] - lb[3]) / g + 1e-9)
        if ix_lo > ix_hi or iy_lo > iy_hi:
            return None
        ix = max(ix_lo, min(ix_hi, round(gx / g)))
        iy = max(iy_lo, min(iy_hi, round(gy / g)))
        return (ix * g, iy * g)

    def _prepare_initial_positions(self):
        shifted = 0
        raw_pos = {}
        for i, mod in enumerate(self.snap.modules):
            ox, oy = mod['x'], mod['y']
            box = self._get_box(i, ox, oy)
            if not self._within_board(box):
                nx, ny = self._clamp_to_inner(i, ox, oy)
                raw_pos[i] = (nx, ny)
                shifted += 1
            else:
                raw_pos[i] = (ox, oy)
        self.shifted_count = shifted
        return raw_pos

    def _build_regular_slots(self, raw_pos, fixed_elem):
        max_w = max((b[2] - b[0]) for b in self.bboxes)
        max_h = max((b[3] - b[1]) for b in self.bboxes)

        g = self.p.C
        step_x = max(g, math.ceil((max_w + self.p.B) / g - 1e-9) * g)
        step_y = max(g, math.ceil((max_h + self.p.B) / g - 1e-9) * g)

        usable_w = self.inner_rect[2] - self.inner_rect[0]
        usable_h = self.inner_rect[3] - self.inner_rect[1]

        cols = max(1, int(math.floor((usable_w - max_w) / step_x + 1e-9)) + 1)
        rows = max(1, int(math.floor((usable_h - max_h) / step_y + 1e-9)) + 1)

        if cols * rows < self.n:
            step_x = max(g * 0.25, (usable_w - max_w) / max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(self.n)) - 1))
            step_y = max(g * 0.25, (usable_h - max_h) / max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(self.n)) - 1))
            cols = max(1, int(math.floor((usable_w - max_w) / step_x + 1e-9)) + 1)
            rows = max(1, int(math.floor((usable_h - max_h) / step_y + 1e-9)) + 1)

        total_grid_w = max_w + (cols - 1) * step_x
        total_grid_h = max_h + (rows - 1) * step_y
        start_x = self.inner_rect[0] + max(0.0, (usable_w - total_grid_w) / 2.0) + max_w / 2.0
        start_y = self.inner_rect[1] + max(0.0, (usable_h - total_grid_h) / 2.0) + max_h / 2.0

        slot_centers = []
        for r in range(rows):
            for col in range(cols):
                slot_centers.append((start_x + col * step_x, start_y + r * step_y))

        if len(slot_centers) < self.n:
            raise RuntimeError("Недостаточно установочных позиций на плате для размещения всех элементов.")

        elem_centers = {}
        for i in range(self.n):
            px, py = raw_pos[i]
            cx, cy = self.center_offsets[i]
            elem_centers[i] = (px + cx, py + cy)

        assignment = [-1] * len(slot_centers)
        used_slots = set()

        order = list(range(self.n))
        if fixed_elem is not None:
            order.remove(fixed_elem)
            order.insert(0, fixed_elem)

        for e in order:
            ecx, ecy = elem_centers[e]
            best_s = min(
                (s for s in range(len(slot_centers)) if s not in used_slots),
                key=lambda s: (math.hypot(slot_centers[s][0] - ecx, slot_centers[s][1] - ecy), s),
            )
            assignment[best_s] = e
            used_slots.add(best_s)

        active_slots = sorted(used_slots)
        if len(slot_centers) > self.n:
            extra_limit = min(len(slot_centers), self.n + max(4, self.n // 2))
            remaining = [s for s in range(len(slot_centers)) if s not in used_slots]
            active_slots.extend(remaining[: max(0, extra_limit - len(active_slots))])

        compact_centers = [slot_centers[s] for s in active_slots]
        compact_assignment = [assignment[s] for s in active_slots]
        return compact_centers, compact_assignment

    def _apply_assignment(self, slot_centers, assignment, fixed_elem, raw_pos):
        placed = {}
        if fixed_elem is not None:
            fx, fy = raw_pos[fixed_elem]
            snapped = self._snap_near(fixed_elem, fx, fy)
            placed[fixed_elem] = snapped if snapped is not None else (fx, fy)

        for s_idx, elem in enumerate(assignment):
            if elem < 0 or elem == fixed_elem:
                continue
            scx, scy = slot_centers[s_idx]
            ox, oy = self.center_offsets[elem]
            gx, gy = self._clamp_to_inner(elem, scx - ox, scy - oy)
            snapped = self._snap_near(elem, gx, gy)
            cand = snapped if snapped is not None else (gx, gy)
            box = self._get_box(elem, *cand)
            if self._within_board(box) and not self._collides_with(box, placed):
                placed[elem] = cand
            else:
                placed[elem] = self._find_nearest_free(elem, gx, gy, placed)

        self.positions = placed

    def _find_nearest_free(self, i, target_x, target_y, placed):
        g = max(0.25, self.p.C / 2.0)
        ix_lo = math.ceil((self.inner_rect[0] - self.bboxes[i][0]) / g - 1e-9)
        ix_hi = math.floor((self.inner_rect[2] - self.bboxes[i][2]) / g + 1e-9)
        iy_lo = math.ceil((self.inner_rect[1] - self.bboxes[i][1]) / g - 1e-9)
        iy_hi = math.floor((self.inner_rect[3] - self.bboxes[i][3]) / g + 1e-9)

        best_pos = None
        best_dist = float('inf')
        for ix in range(ix_lo, ix_hi + 1):
            gx = ix * g
            for iy in range(iy_lo, iy_hi + 1):
                gy = iy * g
                box = self._get_box(i, gx, gy)
                if self._within_board(box) and not self._collides_with(box, placed):
                    d = math.hypot(gx - target_x, gy - target_y)
                    if d < best_dist:
                        best_dist = d
                        best_pos = (gx, gy)
        if best_pos is None:
            ref = self.snap.modules[i]['reference']
            raise RuntimeError(f"Не удалось разместить корпус {ref} без пересечений с заданным зазором B.")
        return best_pos

    def _get_pad_abs(self, i, pad_num):
        mod = self.snap.modules[i]
        lx, ly = mod['pads'][pad_num]
        if i in self.positions:
            gx, gy = self.positions[i]
            return (gx + lx, gy + ly)
        rx, ry = _rotate_offset(lx, ly, mod.get('angle', 0.0))
        return (mod['x'] + rx, mod['y'] + ry)

    def _get_net_pins(self):
        nets = {}
        for i, mod in enumerate(self.snap.modules):
            pads = mod['pads']
            for pad_num, pad_net in mod['pad_nets'].items():
                if pad_net.net_code <= 0 or not pad_net.net_name:
                    continue
                if pad_num not in pads:
                    continue
                nets.setdefault(pad_net.net_code, []).append((mod['reference'], pad_num, i))
        for code in nets:
            nets[code].sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
        return nets

    def _calc_total_wire(self, metric):
        total = 0.0
        for pins in self._get_net_pins().values():
            if len(pins) >= 2:
                pts = [self._get_pad_abs(i, pad_num) for _, pad_num, i in pins]
                total += _mst_length(pts, metric)
        return total

    def _opt_step(self):
        if self.p.optimization_mode == OptimizationMode.D_C:
            return self.p.C
        if self.p.optimization_mode == OptimizationMode.D_C2:
            return self.p.C / 2.0
        if self.p.optimization_mode == OptimizationMode.D_C4:
            return self.p.C / 4.0
        return 0.0

    def _run_extra_optimization(self, fixed_elem):
        d = self._opt_step()
        if d <= 0:
            return
        net_pins = self._get_net_pins()
        active_nets = {code: pins for code, pins in net_pins.items() if len(pins) >= 2}
        if not active_nets:
            return

        mod_nets = [[] for _ in range(self.n)]
        net_lengths = {}
        for code, pins in active_nets.items():
            pts = [self._get_pad_abs(m_idx, pad_num) for _, pad_num, m_idx in pins]
            net_lengths[code] = _mst_length(pts, self.p.metric)
            seen = set()
            for _, _, m_idx in pins:
                if m_idx not in seen:
                    seen.add(m_idx)
                    mod_nets[m_idx].append(code)

        directions = ((d, 0.0), (-d, 0.0), (0.0, d), (0.0, -d))
        for _ in range(500):
            improved = False
            for i in range(self.n):
                if fixed_elem is not None and i == fixed_elem:
                    continue
                incident = mod_nets[i]
                if not incident:
                    continue
                cur_x, cur_y = self.positions[i]
                cur_sub = sum(net_lengths[code] for code in incident)

                for dx, dy in directions:
                    while True:
                        nx = round((cur_x + dx) / d) * d
                        ny = round((cur_y + dy) / d) * d
                        box = self._get_box(i, nx, ny)
                        if not self._within_board(box) or self._collides_with(box, self.positions, i):
                            break
                        self.positions[i] = (nx, ny)
                        cand_lens = {}
                        cand_sub = 0.0
                        for code in incident:
                            pts = [self._get_pad_abs(m_idx, pad_num) for _, pad_num, m_idx in active_nets[code]]
                            nl = _mst_length(pts, self.p.metric)
                            cand_lens[code] = nl
                            cand_sub += nl
                        if cand_sub < cur_sub - 1e-9:
                            cur_x, cur_y = nx, ny
                            cur_sub = cand_sub
                            for code, nl in cand_lens.items():
                                net_lengths[code] = nl
                            improved = True
                        else:
                            self.positions[i] = (cur_x, cur_y)
                            break

            if not improved:
                break

    def run(self):
        board_width = self.edge_rect[2] - self.edge_rect[0]
        board_length = self.edge_rect[3] - self.edge_rect[1]
        if self.n == 0:
            return PairwiseResult(
                {}, 0.0, 0, 0, 0.0, 0.0, "",
                board_width, board_length, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0,
            )

        pair_start = time.perf_counter()

        raw_pos = self._prepare_initial_positions()
        fixed_elem = algo.pick_directive_element(self.bboxes, self.c, self.p.directive_mode.value)
        slot_centers, assignment = self._build_regular_slots(raw_pos, fixed_elem)

        metric_name = "manhattan" if self.p.metric == Metric.MANHATTAN else "euclidean"
        if self.p.algo_version == PairwiseAlgoVersion.SAPRYKIN:
            final_assignment = algo.solve_saprykin(
                self.c, slot_centers, assignment, fixed_elem, metric_name
            )
        else:
            final_assignment = algo.solve_selyutin(
                self.c, slot_centers, assignment, fixed_elem, metric_name
            )

        self._apply_assignment(slot_centers, final_assignment, fixed_elem, raw_pos)

        pair_duration = time.perf_counter() - pair_start

        pair_w_euc = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.EUCLIDEAN)
        pair_w_man = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.MANHATTAN)

        if self.p.optimization_mode != OptimizationMode.NONE:
            opt_start = time.perf_counter()
            self._run_extra_optimization(fixed_elem)
            opt_duration = time.perf_counter() - opt_start
            w_euc = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.EUCLIDEAN)
            w_man = self._calc_total_wire(Metric.MANHATTAN)
        else:
            opt_duration = 0.0
            w_euc = pair_w_euc
            w_man = pair_w_man

        total_duration = pair_duration + opt_duration
        conn_count = sum(len(v) - 1 for v in self._get_net_pins().values() if len(v) >= 2)
        directive_ref = self.snap.modules[fixed_elem]['reference'] if fixed_elem is not None else ""

        return PairwiseResult(
            self.positions,
            total_duration,
            self.n,
            conn_count,
            w_euc,
            w_man,
            directive_ref,
            board_width,
            board_length,
            pair_w_euc,
            pair_w_man,
            pair_duration,
            opt_duration,
            self.shifted_count,
        )


def run_pairwise_placement(board, params):
    snapshot = _BoardSnapshot(board)
    before_euc, before_man = _snapshot_wire_lengths(snapshot)
    result = PairwisePlacer(snapshot, params).run()
    return result, before_euc, before_man.
.
============================================================
FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\core\paiwise_algorithm.py
============================================================
import math
import random


def calc_connectivity_matrix(snapshot):
    n = len(snapshot.modules)
    c = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    nets = {}
    for i, mod in enumerate(snapshot.modules):
        for pad_net in mod['pad_nets'].values():
            if pad_net.net_code <= 0 or not pad_net.net_name:
                continue
            nets.setdefault(pad_net.net_code, set()).add(i)
    for mod_set in nets.values():
        m_list = list(mod_set)
        for idx1 in range(len(m_list)):
            for idx2 in range(idx1 + 1, len(m_list)):
                u, v = m_list[idx1], m_list[idx2]
                c[u][v] += 1.0
                c[v][u] += 1.0
    return c


def pick_directive_element(bboxes, c, mode):
    if mode == "none":
        return None
    n = len(bboxes)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    if mode == "largest":
        return max(
            range(n),
            key=lambda i: ((bboxes[i][2] - bboxes[i][0]) * (bboxes[i][3] - bboxes[i][1]), -i),
        )
    return max(range(n), key=lambda i: (sum(c[i]), -i))


def distance(p1, p2, metric_name):
    dx = abs(p1[0] - p2[0])
    dy = abs(p1[1] - p2[1])
    if metric_name == "manhattan":
        return dx + dy
    return math.hypot(dx, dy)


def _node_distances(nodes, metric_name):
    m = len(nodes)
    d = [[0.0] * m for _ in range(m)]
    for i in range(m):
        for j in range(i + 1, m):
            dist = distance(nodes[i], nodes[j], metric_name)
            d[i][j] = dist
            d[j][i] = dist
    return d


def solve_saprykin(c, nodes, assignment, fixed_elem, metric_name, max_iters=200):
    n = len(c)
    m = len(nodes)
    d_mat = _node_distances(nodes, metric_name)

    r = [[0.0] * m for _ in range(m)]
    for u in range(m):
        eu = assignment[u]
        if eu < 0:
            continue
        for v in range(u + 1, m):
            ev = assignment[v]
            if ev < 0:
                continue
            val = c[eu][ev]
            r[u][v] = val
            r[v][u] = val

    p = [[0.0] * m for _ in range(m)]
    for i in range(m):
        for j in range(m):
            s = 0.0
            for k in range(m):
                s += r[i][k] * d_mat[j][k]
            p[i][j] = s

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        candidates = []
        for i in range(m):
            if fixed_elem is not None and assignment[i] == fixed_elem:
                continue
            for j in range(i + 1, m):
                if fixed_elem is not None and assignment[j] == fixed_elem:
                    continue
                if assignment[i] < 0 and assignment[j] < 0:
                    continue
                q_ij = (p[i][j] - p[i][i]) + (p[j][i] - p[j][j])
                dl_ij = 2.0 * r[i][j] * d_mat[i][j] + q_ij
                if dl_ij < -1e-9:
                    candidates.append((dl_ij, i, j))

        if not candidates:
            break

        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1], x[2]))

        selected = []
        used_nodes = set()
        for dl_ij, i, j in candidates:
            if i in used_nodes or j in used_nodes:
                continue
            if any(r[i][u] > 0 or r[i][v] > 0 or r[j][u] > 0 or r[j][v] > 0 for u, v in selected):
                continue
            selected.append((i, j))
            used_nodes.add(i)
            used_nodes.add(j)

        if not selected:
            break

        for i, j in selected:
            assignment[i], assignment[j] = assignment[j], assignment[i]
            for k in range(m):
                r[i][k], r[j][k] = r[j][k], r[i][k]
            for k in range(m):
                r[k][i], r[k][j] = r[k][j], r[k][i]

        for i in range(m):
            for j in range(m):
                s = 0.0
                for k in range(m):
                    s += r[i][k] * d_mat[j][k]
                p[i][j] = s

    return assignment


def solve_selyutin(c, nodes, assignment, fixed_elem, metric_name, max_iters=200):
    n = len(c)
    m = len(nodes)
    d_mat = _node_distances(nodes, metric_name)

    elem_to_node = [-1] * n
    for u, e in enumerate(assignment):
        if e >= 0:
            elem_to_node[e] = u

    order = sorted(range(n), key=lambda e: (-sum(c[e]), e))

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        improved_any = False
        for idx_k, ei in enumerate(order):
            if fixed_elem is not None and ei == fixed_elem:
                continue
            h = elem_to_node[ei]

            best_df = 0.0
            best_target_node = -1

            for idx_m in range(idx_k + 1, n):
                ej = order[idx_m]
                if fixed_elem is not None and ej == fixed_elem:
                    continue
                k = elem_to_node[ej]

                df = 0.0
                for s in range(n):
                    if s == ei or s == ej:
                        continue
                    ps = elem_to_node[s]
                    df += (c[ei][s] - c[ej][s]) * (d_mat[h][ps] - d_mat[k][ps])

                if df > best_df + 1e-9:
                    best_df = df
                    best_target_node = k

            for u in range(m):
                if assignment[u] != -1:
                    continue
                df = 0.0
                for s in range(n):
                    if s == ei:
                        continue
                    ps = elem_to_node[s]
                    df += c[ei][s] * (d_mat[h][ps] - d_mat[u][ps])
                if df > best_df + 1e-9:
                    best_df = df
                    best_target_node = u

            if best_target_node != -1:
                k = best_target_node
                ej = assignment[k]
                assignment[h] = ej
                assignment[k] = ei
                elem_to_node[ei] = k
                if ej >= 0:
                    elem_to_node[ej] = h
                improved_any = True

        if not improved_any:
            break

    return assignment.

import math


def build_mst_edges(points):
    n = len(points)
    if n < 2:
        return []

    in_tree = [False] * n
    in_tree[0] = True
    edges = []

    best_dist = [float('inf')] * n
    best_from = [0] * n
    for j in range(1, n):
        dx = points[j][0] - points[0][0]
        dy = points[j][1] - points[0][1]
        best_dist[j] = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        best_from[j] = 0

    for _ in range(n - 1):
        u = -1
        best = float('inf')
        for j in range(n):
            if not in_tree[j] and best_dist[j] < best:
                best = best_dist[j]
                u = j
        if u == -1:
            break

        edges.append((best_from[u], u))
        in_tree[u] = True

        ux, uy = points[u][0], points[u][1]
        for v in range(n):
            if in_tree[v]:
                continue
            vx, vy = points[v][0], points[v][1]
            d = math.hypot(vx - ux, vy - uy)
            if d < best_dist[v]:
                best_dist[v] = d
                best_from[v] = u

    return edges.

class JMethod:
    M_331 = "3.3.1"
    M_333 = "3.3.3"
    M_335 = "3.3.5"
    M_336 = "3.3.6"


def calc_J_331(c_331, i, placed):
    best = 0.0
    for j in placed:
        w = c_331[i][j]
        if w > best:
            best = w
    return best


def calc_J_333(c, i, placed):
    s = 0.0
    for j in placed:
        s += c[i][j]
    return s


def calc_J_335(c, i, placed, unplaced):
    placed_sum = 0.0
    unplaced_sum = 0.0
    ci = c[i]
    for j in placed:
        placed_sum += ci[j]
    for j in unplaced:
        if j != i:
            unplaced_sum += ci[j]
    return placed_sum - unplaced_sum


def calc_J_336(c, i, placed, unplaced, n):
    placed_sum = 0.0
    total_sum = 0.0
    ci = c[i]
    for j in range(n):
        if j == i:
            continue
        total_sum += ci[j]
        if j in placed:
            placed_sum += ci[j]
    return (placed_sum / total_sum) if total_sum > 0 else 0.0


def calc_J(c, c_331, j_method, i, placed, unplaced, n):
    if j_method == JMethod.M_331:
        return calc_J_331(c_331, i, placed)
    if j_method == JMethod.M_333:
        return calc_J_333(c, i, placed)
    if j_method == JMethod.M_335:
        return calc_J_335(c, i, placed, unplaced)
    if j_method == JMethod.M_336:
        return calc_J_336(c, i, placed, unplaced, n)
    return 0.0


def pick_next_element(c, c_331, j_method, unplaced, placed, n):
    if not unplaced:
        return None, 0.0
    scores = {i: calc_J(c, c_331, j_method, i, placed, unplaced, n) for i in unplaced}
    best_i = max(scores, key=lambda x: (scores[x], -x))
    return best_i, scores[best_i]


def pick_best_position(pi, placed_positions, metric):
    gx, gy = pi
    total = 0.0
    if metric == "manhattan":
        for jx, jy, w in placed_positions:
            if w == 0:
                continue
            total += w * (abs(gx - jx) + abs(gy - jy))
    else:
        for jx, jy, w in placed_positions:
            if w == 0:
                continue
            dx = gx - jx
            dy = gy - jy
            total += w * (dx * dx + dy * dy) ** 0.5
    return total


def choose_best_candidate(element_index, candidates, placed_positions, metric):
    best_pos = None
    best_f = float("inf")
    for pi in candidates:
        f = pick_best_position(pi, placed_positions, metric)
        if f < best_f or (f == best_f and (best_pos is None or pi < best_pos)):
            best_f = f
            best_pos = pi
    return best_pos, best_f


def _is_power_net(name):
    if not name:
        return False
    up = name.upper()
    for prefix in ("GND", "VCC", "VDD", "VSS", "AGND", "DGND",
                   "+5V", "+3V3", "+3.3V", "+12V", "-5V", "-12V"):
        if up.startswith(prefix):
            return True
    return False


def compute_omega(snapshot):
    n = len(snapshot.modules)
    if n <= 1:
        return {}

    nets_modules = {}
    net_names = {}
    for i, mod in enumerate(snapshot.modules):
        for pad_net in mod['pad_nets'].values():
            if pad_net.net_code > 0:
                nets_modules.setdefault(pad_net.net_code, set()).add(i)
                net_names[pad_net.net_code] = pad_net.net_name

    omega = {}
    for code, mods in nets_modules.items():
        if _is_power_net(net_names.get(code, "")):
            omega[code] = 0.3
        else:
            omega[code] = 1.0 - len(mods) / n
    return omega


def compute_connectivity_matrix(snapshot, lmbda=2):
    n = len(snapshot.modules)
    omega = compute_omega(snapshot)

    nets_by_mod = [set() for _ in range(n)]
    for i, mod in enumerate(snapshot.modules):
        for pad_num, pad_net in mod['pad_nets'].items():
            if pad_net.net_code > 0:
                nets_by_mod[i].add(pad_net.net_code)

    net_sizes = {}
    for nets in nets_by_mod:
        for net in nets:
            net_sizes[net] = net_sizes.get(net, 0) + 1

    c = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    c_331 = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]

    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            common = nets_by_mod[i] & nets_by_mod[j]
            if common:
                c[i][j] = c[j][i] = float(len(common))
                c_331[i][j] = c_331[j][i] = sum(
                    ((net_sizes[code] + lmbda) / net_sizes[code]) * omega.get(code, 1.0)
                    for code in common
                )
    return c, c_331


def pick_directive_element(bboxes, c, mode, directive_position):
    scores = []
    for i in range(len(bboxes)):
        if mode == "largest":
            lb = bboxes[i]
            score = (lb[2] - lb[0]) * (lb[3] - lb[1])
        else:
            score = sum(c[i])
        scores.append((i, score))
    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    return scores[0][0].
.
