from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker, QBrush
from PySide6.QtCore import QLineF, QRectF, QPointF, Qt

from constants import Colors
from constants.board_settings import LAYER_COLORS


def _layer_color(layer):
    if layer in LAYER_COLORS:
        return QColor(*LAYER_COLORS[layer])
    return QColor(*Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE)


def _snap(value, grid):
    if grid <= 0:
        return value
    return round(value / grid) * grid


class BoardLineGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    HANDLE_RADIUS_PX = 4.0
    HOVER_RADIUS_PX = 5.0
    ACTIVE_RADIUS_PX = 6.0

    def __init__(self, line, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = line
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self

        self._active_handle = None
        self._hover_handle = None

        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, False)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setAcceptHoverEvents(True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(0.0, 0.0)

    def get_handles(self):
        return [QPointF(self.model.x1, self.model.y1),
                QPointF(self.model.x2, self.model.y2)]

    def _hit_test_handle(self, scene_pos):
        if not self.isSelected():
            return None
        scale = self.canvas.transform().m11() if self.canvas else 1.0
        if scale <= 0:
            scale = 1.0
        radius = self.HANDLE_RADIUS_PX * 3.0 / scale
        best_idx = None
        best_dist = float('inf')
        for i, pt in enumerate(self.get_handles()):
            dx = scene_pos.x() - pt.x()
            dy = scene_pos.y() - pt.y()
            dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy) ** 0.5
            if dist < radius and dist < best_dist:
                best_idx = i
                best_dist = dist
        return best_idx

    def boundingRect(self):
        w = self.model.width + 0.5
        rect = QRectF(self.model.x1, self.model.y1,
                      self.model.x2 - self.model.x1,
                      self.model.y2 - self.model.y1).normalized()
        return rect.adjusted(-w, -w, w, w)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(self.model.x1, self.model.y1)
        path.lineTo(self.model.x2, self.model.y2)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.4, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        selected = bool(option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected)
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if selected else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        pen = QPen(color, max(0.05, self.model.width))
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QLineF(self.model.x1, self.model.y1,
                                self.model.x2, self.model.y2))

        if selected:
            scale = self.canvas.transform().m11() if self.canvas else 1.0
            if scale <= 0:
                scale = 1.0
            for i, pt in enumerate(self.get_handles()):
                active = (i == self._active_handle)
                hover = (i == self._hover_handle)
                if active:
                    fill = QColor(0, 200, 0)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    r_px = self.ACTIVE_RADIUS_PX
                elif hover:
                    fill = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    border = QColor(0, 150, 255)
                    r_px = self.HOVER_RADIUS_PX
                else:
                    fill = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    r_px = self.HANDLE_RADIUS_PX
                radius = r_px / scale
                p = QPen(border, 0)
                p.setCosmetic(True)
                painter.setPen(p)
                painter.setBrush(QBrush(fill))
                painter.drawEllipse(pt, radius, radius)

    def hoverMoveEvent(self, event):
        scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
        idx = self._hit_test_handle(scene_pos)
        if idx != self._hover_handle:
            self._hover_handle = idx
            self.update()
        if idx is not None:
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.SizeAllCursor)
        else:
            self.unsetCursor()
        super().hoverMoveEvent(event)

    def hoverLeaveEvent(self, event):
        if self._hover_handle is not None:
            self._hover_handle = None
            self.update()
        self.unsetCursor()
        super().hoverLeaveEvent(event)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            handle_idx = self._hit_test_handle(scene_pos)
            if handle_idx is not None:
                self._active_handle = handle_idx
                self.update()
                event.accept()
                return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._active_handle is not None and (event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton):
            current = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            sx = _snap(current.x(), grid)
            sy = _snap(current.y(), grid)
            if self._active_handle == 0:
                self.model.x1 = sx
                self.model.y1 = sy
            else:
                self.model.x2 = sx
                self.model.y2 = sy
            self.prepareGeometryChange()
            self.update()
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if self._active_handle is not None:
            self._active_handle = None
            self.update()
            if hasattr(self.canvas, 'content_changed'):
                self.canvas.content_changed.emit()
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)


class BoardCircleGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, circle, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = circle
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(circle.x, circle.y)

    def boundingRect(self):
        r = self.model.radius
        extra = self.model.width + 0.5
        return QRectF(-r, -r, 2 * r, 2 * r).adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addEllipse(QPointF(0.0, 0.0), self.model.radius, self.model.radius)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.4, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if (option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected) else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        pen = QPen(color, max(0.05, self.model.width))
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(0.0, 0.0), self.model.radius, self.model.radius)

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            self.model.x = value.x()
            self.model.y = value.y()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value)


class BoardArcGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, arc, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = arc
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(arc.x, arc.y)

    def _local_path(self):
        pts = arc_sample_points(
            0.0, 0.0,
            self.model.sx - self.model.x,
            self.model.sy - self.model.y,
            self.model.angle, 64,
        )
        if len(pts) < 2:
            return QPainterPath()
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
        for px, py in pts[1:]:
            path.lineTo(px, py)
        return path

    def boundingRect(self):
        b = self._local_path().boundingRect()
        extra = self.model.width + 0.5
        return b.adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.4, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(self._local_path())

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if (option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected) else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        pen = QPen(color, max(0.05, self.model.width))
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawPath(self._local_path())

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            dx = value.x() - self.model.x
            dy = value.y() - self.model.y
            self.model.x = value.x()
            self.model.y = value.y()
            self.model.sx += dx
            self.model.sy += dy
            self.prepareGeometryChange()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value)


class BoardTextGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, text, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = text
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(text.x, text.y)
        self._path_cache = None
        self._path_key = None

    def _path(self):
        target_h = self.model.size * Sizes.TEXT_FONT_MULTIPLIER
        key = (self.model.text, target_h)
        if self._path_key != key:
            self._path_cache = build_text_path(self.model.text, target_h, center=True)
            self._path_key = key
        return self._path_cache

    def boundingRect(self):
        path = self._path()
        if path.isEmpty():
            return QRectF(-1, -1, 2, 2)
        b = path.boundingRect()
        return QRectF(b.left(), b.top(), b.width(), b.height()).adjusted(-1, -1, 1, 1)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        path = self._path()
        if path.isEmpty():
            return
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if (option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected) else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        painter.save()
        painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        painter.setBrush(color)
        painter.drawPath(path)
        painter.restore()

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            self.model.x = value.x()
            self.model.y = value.y()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value).
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\graphics\module_item.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QBrush, QColor, QPainterPath, QTransform
from PySide6.QtCore import QRectF, QPointF, Qt

from constants import Colors, Sizes
from common.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
from common.graphics.text_path import build_text_path
from pbe_editor.core.models import PadShape, PadType, TextType


STANDARD_COLOR = (180, 160, 80)
SMD_COLOR = (220, 90, 90)
CONN_COLOR = (235, 150, 60)


class BoardModuleGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, module, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.module = module
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.module.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, False)
        self.setPos(module.x, module.y)
        self.setRotation(module.angle)
        self.setZValue(10)
        self._drag_start_scene = None
        self._drag_start_pos = None
        self._drag_moved = False
        self._is_drag_leader = False
        self._group_drag_start = {}

    def _show_values(self):
        return bool(getattr(self.canvas, 'show_values', False))

    def _text_visible(self, text):
        if text.text_type == TextType.VALUE:
            return self._show_values()
        return text.visible

    def boundingRect(self):
        fp = self.module.footprint
        xs = []
        ys = []
        for pad in fp.pads:
            xs.append(pad.x - pad.width / 2)
            xs.append(pad.x + pad.width / 2)
            ys.append(pad.y - pad.height / 2)
            ys.append(pad.y + pad.height / 2)
        for line in fp.lines:
            xs.extend([line.x, line.x2])
            ys.extend([line.y, line.y2])
        for circle in fp.circles:
            xs.extend([circle.x - circle.radius, circle.x + circle.radius])
            ys.extend([circle.y - circle.radius, circle.y + circle.radius])
        for arc in fp.arcs:
            for px, py in arc_sample_points(arc.x, arc.y, arc.sx, arc.sy, arc.angle, 32):
                xs.append(px)
                ys.append(py)
        for text in fp.texts:
            if self._text_visible(text):
                bounds = self._text_path(text).boundingRect()
                xs.extend([bounds.left(), bounds.right()])
                ys.extend([bounds.top(), bounds.bottom()])
        if not xs:
            return QRectF(-10, -10, 20, 20)
        left = min(xs); right = max(xs); top = min(ys); bottom = max(ys)
        extra = 1.0
        return QRectF(left - extra, top - extra,
                      right - left + 2 * extra, bottom - top + 2 * extra)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addRect(self.boundingRect())
        return path

    def _pad_color(self, pad, selected):
        if selected:
            return QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        if pad.pad_type == PadType.SMD:
            return QColor(*SMD_COLOR)
        if pad.pad_type == PadType.CONN:
            return QColor(*CONN_COLOR)
        return QColor(*STANDARD_COLOR)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        fp = self.module.footprint
        selected = bool(option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected)
        if selected:
            outline_color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        else:
            outline_color = QColor(200, 200, 200)
        pen = QPen(outline_color, 0.15)
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        for line in fp.lines:
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(line.x, line.y), QPointF(line.x2, line.y2))
        for circle in fp.circles:
            painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(circle.x, circle.y), circle.radius, circle.radius)
        for arc in fp.arcs:
            pts = arc_sample_points(arc.x, arc.y, arc.sx, arc.sy, arc.angle, 32)
            if len(pts) >= 2:
                path = QPainterPath()
                path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
                for px, py in pts[1:]:
                    path.lineTo(px, py)
                painter.drawPath(path)
        for pad in fp.pads:
            pad_color = self._pad_color(pad, selected)
            w = pad.width; h = pad.height
            rect = QRectF(pad.x - w / 2, pad.y - h / 2, w, h)
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(pad_color)
            if pad.shape == PadShape.RECT:
                painter.drawRect(rect)
            elif pad.shape == PadShape.OVAL:
                painter.drawRoundedRect(rect, w / 2, h / 2)
            else:
                painter.drawEllipse(rect)
            drill = pad.drill or 0.0
            if drill > 0:
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND))
                painter.drawEllipse(QRectF(pad.x - drill / 2, pad.y - drill / 2, drill, drill))
            if pad.number:
                target_h = min(pad.width, pad.height) * Sizes.PAD_NUMBER_FONT_RATIO
                text_path = build_text_path(pad.number, target_h, bold=True, center=True)
                painter.save()
                painter.translate(pad.x, pad.y)
                painter.rotate(-self.module.angle)
                painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.PAD_NUMBER))
                painter.drawPath(text_path)
                painter.restore()
        for text in fp.texts:
            if not self._text_visible(text):
                continue
            path = self._text_path(text)
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            if selected:
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.SELECTED))
            elif text.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE:
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.REFERENCE))
            elif text.text_type == TextType.VALUE:
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.VALUE))
            else:
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*(Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE if text.visible else Colors.TEXT_HIDDEN)))
            painter.drawPath(path)

    def _text_path(self, text):
        value = (self.module.reference if text.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE else
                 self.module.value if text.text_type == TextType.VALUE else text.text)
        path = build_text_path(value, text.size, bold=True, center=True)
        transform = QTransform()
        transform.translate(text.x, text.y)
        return transform.map(path)

    def _collect_selected_modules(self):
        self._group_drag_start = {}
        scene = self.scene()
        if scene is None:
            return
        for item in scene.selectedItems():
            if isinstance(item, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                self._group_drag_start[item] = (item.pos().x(), item.pos().y())

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)
            if self.isSelected():
                self._drag_start_scene = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
                self._drag_start_pos = QPointF(self.pos())
                self._drag_moved = False
                self._is_drag_leader = True
                self._collect_selected_modules()
                event.accept()
                return
        else:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._is_drag_leader and self._drag_start_scene is not None and \
                (event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton):
            current = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            delta = current - self._drag_start_scene
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            snapped_dx = round(delta.x() / grid) * grid
            snapped_dy = round(delta.y() / grid) * grid
            if snapped_dx != 0 or snapped_dy != 0:
                self._drag_moved = True
            for item, (sx, sy) in self._group_drag_start.items():
                new_x = sx + snapped_dx
                new_y = sy + snapped_dy
                item.setPos(new_x, new_y)
                item.module.x = new_x
                item.module.y = new_y
            if hasattr(self.canvas, 'ratsnest_update_requested'):
                self.canvas.ratsnest_update_requested.emit()
            self.canvas.viewport().update()
            event.accept()
        else:
            self._drag_start_scene = None
            super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        was_dragging = self._drag_moved
        self._drag_start_scene = None
        self._drag_start_pos = None
        self._drag_moved = False
        self._is_drag_leader = False
        self._group_drag_start = {}
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)
        if was_dragging and hasattr(self.canvas, 'ratsnest_update_requested'):
            self.canvas.ratsnest_update_requested.emit()

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker
from PySide6.QtCore import QLineF, QRectF, QPointF, Qt

from constants import Colors


class RatsnestGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    _next_uid = [1]

    def __init__(self, p1: QPointF, p2: QPointF, net_code: int = 0,
                 net_name: str = "", is_power: bool = False,
                 is_gnd: bool = False,
                 label_a: str = "", label_b: str = "", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.p1 = QPointF(p1)
        self.p2 = QPointF(p2)
        self.net_code = net_code
        self.net_name = net_name
        self.is_power = is_power
        self.is_gnd = is_gnd
        self.label_a = label_a
        self.label_b = label_b
        self.uid = RatsnestGraphicsItem._next_uid[0]
        RatsnestGraphicsItem._next_uid[0] += 1
        self.setZValue(1)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton | Qt.MouseButton.RightButton)

    def boundingRect(self):
        rect = QRectF(self.p1, self.p2).normalized()
        return rect.adjusted(-0.5, -0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(self.p1)
        path.lineTo(self.p2)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(0.6)
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        if option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected:
            color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        elif self.is_gnd:
            color = QColor(220, 70, 70)
        elif self.is_power:
            color = QColor(235, 150, 60)
        else:
            color = QColor(80, 200, 120)
        pen = QPen(color, 0)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QLineF(self.p1, self.p2)).
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\ui\board_canvas.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsView, QGraphicsScene, QGraphicsItem
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPointF, Signal, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QWheelEvent, QMouseEvent, QPainter, QPen, QPainterPath

from constants import Colors, Sizes, Steps, VK_W, VK_A, VK_S, VK_D
from common.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
from common.graphics.text_path import build_text_path


class BoardCanvas(QGraphicsView):
    zoom_changed = Signal(float)
    mouse_moved = Signal(float, float)
    ratsnest_update_requested = Signal()
    canvas_clicked = Signal(float, float)
    canvas_right_clicked = Signal()
    selection_changed = Signal()
    content_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._panning = False
        self._pan_start = None
        self.show_grid = True
        self.show_snap = True
        self.show_values = False
        self._highlight_pad_pos = None
        self._highlight_pad_radius = 0.0

        self.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.viewport().setMouseTracking(True)
        self.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag)
        self.setRubberBandSelectionMode(Qt.ItemSelectionMode.IntersectsItemShape)
        self.setViewportUpdateMode(QGraphicsView.ViewportUpdateMode.FullViewportUpdate)
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        self.setFrameShape(QGraphicsView.Shape.Box)
        self.setFrameShadow(QGraphicsView.Shadow.Plain)
        self.setLineWidth(1)
        self.setStyleSheet("QGraphicsView { border: 1px solid #ffffff; }")
        self.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.StrongFocus)

        self.scene = QGraphicsScene(self)
        self.setScene(self.scene)
        self.scene.setSceneRect(-2000, -2000, 4000, 4000)
        self.scene.selectionChanged.connect(self.selection_changed.emit)

        self.grid_size = float(Steps.DEFAULT_GRID)
        self.grid_color = QColor(*Colors.GRID)
        self.background_color = QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(self.background_color)

        self.base_scale = Sizes.BASE_SCALE / 4.0
        self.scale(self.base_scale, self.base_scale)
        self.zoom_steps = Steps.ZOOM
        self.zoom_index = self.zoom_steps.index(1.0)
        self.zoom_level = 1.0

        self._snap_pos = None

        self.pressed_keys = set()
        self.pan_speed_px = Sizes.PAN_SPEED_PX
        self.pan_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.pan_timer.setInterval(Sizes.PAN_TIMER_INTERVAL)
        self.pan_timer.timeout.connect(self._pan_step)
        self._scroll_acc_x = 0.0
        self._scroll_acc_y = 0.0

        self._ghost_line_start = None
        self._ghost_line_end = None
        self._ghost_circle_center = None
        self._ghost_circle_radius = 0.0
        self._ghost_arc = None
        self._ghost_text_pos = None
        self._ghost_text_content = ""
        self._ghost_text_size = 1.0
        self._ghost_text_visible = True

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.grid_color = QColor(*Colors.GRID)
        self.background_color = QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(self.background_color)
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_grid_size(self, size_mm):
        self.grid_size = size_mm
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_show_snap(self, show):
        self.show_snap = show
        if not show:
            self._snap_pos = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_show_values(self, show):
        self.show_values = show
        for item in self.scene.items():
            try:
                item.update()
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_highlight_pad(self, pos, radius):
        self._highlight_pad_pos = QPointF(pos)
        self._highlight_pad_radius = radius
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_highlight_pad(self):
        if self._highlight_pad_pos is not None:
            self._highlight_pad_pos = None
            self._highlight_pad_radius = 0.0
            self.viewport().update()

    def snap_to_grid(self, x, y):
        g = self.grid_size
        return QPointF(round(x / g) * g, round(y / g) * g)

    def set_ghost_line(self, x1, y1, x2, y2):
        self._ghost_line_start = QPointF(x1, y1)
        self._ghost_line_end = QPointF(x2, y2)
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_line(self):
        self._ghost_line_start = None
        self._ghost_line_end = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_circle(self, cx, cy, r):
        self._ghost_circle_center = QPointF(cx, cy)
        self._ghost_circle_radius = r
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_circle(self):
        self._ghost_circle_center = None
        self._ghost_circle_radius = 0.0
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_arc(self, cx, cy, sx, sy, angle):
        self._ghost_arc = (cx, cy, sx, sy, angle)
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_arc(self):
        self._ghost_arc = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_text(self, x, y, text, size, visible):
        self._ghost_text_pos = QPointF(x, y)
        self._ghost_text_content = text
        self._ghost_text_size = size
        self._ghost_text_visible = visible
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_text(self):
        self._ghost_text_pos = None
        self._ghost_text_content = ""
        self._ghost_text_size = 1.0
        self._ghost_text_visible = True
        self.viewport().update()

    def drawBackground(self, painter, rect):
        super().drawBackground(painter, rect)
        if not self.show_grid:
            return
        g = self.grid_size
        scale = self.transform().m11()
        if g * scale < Sizes.MIN_CELL_PX:
            return

        left = int(rect.left() // g) * g
        right = int(rect.right() // g) * g
        top = int(rect.top() // g) * g
        bottom = int(rect.bottom() // g) * g

        step_x = g
        step_y = g
        max_points = 60
        if (right - left) / g > max_points:
            factor = 1
            while (right - left) / (g * factor) > max_points:
                factor *= 2
            step_x = g * factor
        if (bottom - top) / g > max_points:
            factor = 1
            while (bottom - top) / (g * factor) > max_points:
                factor *= 2
            step_y = g * factor

        pen = QPen(self.grid_color, 0)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        y = top
        while y <= bottom:
            x = left
            while x <= right:
                painter.drawPoint(QPointF(x, y))
                x += step_x
            y += step_y

    def drawForeground(self, painter, rect):
        super().drawForeground(painter, rect)
        pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.AXIS), 0)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QPointF(rect.left(), 0), QPointF(rect.right(), 0))
        painter.drawLine(QPointF(0, rect.top()), QPointF(0, rect.bottom()))

        if self._snap_pos and self.show_snap:
            cross = Sizes.CROSS_SIZE / self.transform().m11()
            p = QPen(QColor(*Colors.SNAP_CROSS), 0)
            p.setCosmetic(True)
            painter.setPen(p)
            x = self._snap_pos.x()
            y = self._snap_pos.y()
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(x - cross, y), QPointF(x + cross, y))
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(x, y - cross), QPointF(x, y + cross))

        if self._highlight_pad_pos is not None and self._highlight_pad_radius > 0:
            hp = QPen(QColor(255, 200, 0), 0)
            hp.setCosmetic(True)
            hp.setWidth(2)
            painter.setPen(hp)
            painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
            painter.drawEllipse(self._highlight_pad_pos,
                                self._highlight_pad_radius,
                                self._highlight_pad_radius)

        ghost_pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.GHOST_LINE), 0)
        ghost_pen.setCosmetic(True)
        ghost_pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
        painter.setPen(ghost_pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)

        if self._ghost_line_start and self._ghost_line_end:
            painter.drawLine(self._ghost_line_start, self._ghost_line_end)

        if self._ghost_circle_center and self._ghost_circle_radius > 0:
            painter.drawEllipse(self._ghost_circle_center,
                                self._ghost_circle_radius,
                                self._ghost_circle_radius)

        if self._ghost_arc is not None:
            cx, cy, sx, sy, angle = self._ghost_arc
            pts = arc_sample_points(cx, cy, sx, sy, angle, 64)
            if len(pts) >= 2:
                path = QPainterPath()
                path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
                for px, py in pts[1:]:
                    path.lineTo(px, py)
                painter.drawPath(path)

        if self._ghost_text_pos and self._ghost_text_content:
            path = build_text_path(
                self._ghost_text_content,
                self._ghost_text_size * Sizes.TEXT_FONT_MULTIPLIER,
                center=True,
            )
            if not path.isEmpty():
                color = QColor(*Colors.GHOST_TEXT)
                if not self._ghost_text_visible:
                    color = color.lighter(50)
                painter.save()
                painter.translate(self._ghost_text_pos)
                painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
                painter.setBrush(color)
                painter.drawPath(path)
                painter.restore()

    def wheelEvent(self, event: QWheelEvent):
        if event.angleDelta().y() > 0:
            if self.zoom_index < len(self.zoom_steps) - 1:
                self.zoom_index += 1
        elif self.zoom_index > 0:
            self.zoom_index -= 1
        new_zoom = self.zoom_steps[self.zoom_index]
        factor = (self.base_scale * new_zoom) / self.transform().m11()
        self.scale(factor, factor)
        self.zoom_level = new_zoom
        self.zoom_changed.emit(self.zoom_level)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton:
            self._panning = True
            self._pan_start = event.position().toPoint()
            self.viewport().setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ClosedHandCursor)
            event.accept()
            return
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.RightButton:
            self.canvas_right_clicked.emit()
            event.accept()
            return
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            if self.dragMode() == QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag:
                items = self.items(event.position().toPoint())
                selectable = [it for it in items if it.flags() & QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable]
                if selectable and (event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier):
                    selectable[0].setSelected(not selectable[0].isSelected())
                    event.accept()
                    return
                if not selectable:
                    scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
                    snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
                    self.canvas_clicked.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
                super().mousePressEvent(event)
                return
            items = self.items(event.position().toPoint())
            selectable = [it for it in items if it.flags() & QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable]
            if not selectable:
                scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
                snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
                self.canvas_clicked.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
                event.accept()
                return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton and self._panning:
            self._panning = False
            self.viewport().unsetCursor()
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if self._panning:
            current_pos = event.position().toPoint()
            delta = current_pos - self._pan_start
            self._pan_start = current_pos
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() - delta.x())
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() - delta.y())
            event.accept()
            return
        scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
        snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
        if self.show_snap:
            self._snap_pos = QPointF(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        else:
            self._snap_pos = None
        self.mouse_moved.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        self.viewport().update()
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def _pan_step(self):
        dx = dy = 0
        if VK_W in self.pressed_keys:
            dy -= 1
        if VK_S in self.pressed_keys:
            dy += 1
        if VK_A in self.pressed_keys:
            dx -= 1
        if VK_D in self.pressed_keys:
            dx += 1
        if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
            return
        self._scroll_acc_x += dx * self.pan_speed_px
        self._scroll_acc_y += dy * self.pan_speed_px
        ix = int(self._scroll_acc_x)
        iy = int(self._scroll_acc_y)
        self._scroll_acc_x -= ix
        self._scroll_acc_y -= iy
        if ix or iy:
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() + ix)
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() + iy).
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\ui\commands\autoplace.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox
from pbe_editor.ui.dialogs.autoplace_dialog import AutoPlaceDialog
from pbe_editor.ui.dialogs.autoplace_result_dialog import AutoPlaceResultDialog
from pbe_editor.core.autoplacer import run_auto_placement


class AutoplaceCommandsMixin:
    def __init__(self):
        self._last_params = None
        self._last_threads = 1

    def _apply_positions(self, positions):
        for i, module in enumerate(self.w.board.modules):
            if i in positions:
                module.x, module.y = positions[i]
                module.angle = 0.0
                item = self.w.manager.items.get(module.uid)
                if item:
                    item.setPos(module.x, module.y)
                    item.setRotation(0.0)
                    item.prepareGeometryChange()
                    item.update()

    def _restore_initial_positions(self, initial_positions):
        for i, (x, y, angle) in initial_positions.items():
            module = self.w.board.modules[i]
            module.x = x
            module.y = y
            module.angle = angle
            item = self.w.manager.items.get(module.uid)
            if item:
                item.setPos(module.x, module.y)
                item.setRotation(angle)
                item.prepareGeometryChange()
                item.update()
        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()

    def on_autoplace(self):
        if not self.w.board.modules:
            QMessageBox.information(self.w, "Информация", "На плате нет модулей для размещения.")
            return

        has_edge = any(line.layer == "Edge.Cuts" for line in self.w.board.lines)
        if not has_edge:
            QMessageBox.warning(self.w, "Ошибка",
                                "На плате не нарисован контур (Edge.Cuts). Авторазмещение невозможно.")
            return

        initial_positions = {i: (m.x, m.y, m.angle) for i, m in enumerate(self.w.board.modules)}

        dlg = AutoPlaceDialog(self.w, self._last_params, self._last_threads)

        while True:
            if dlg.exec() != AutoPlaceDialog.Accepted:
                return

            params = dlg.get_params()
            threads = dlg.get_threads()
            self._last_params = params
            self._last_threads = threads

            self._restore_initial_positions(initial_positions)

            try:
                result, before_euc, before_man = run_auto_placement(self.w.board, params, threads)
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self.w, "Ошибка", str(e))
                self._restore_initial_positions(initial_positions)
                return

            self._apply_positions(result.positions)
            self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
            self.w.commit_snapshot()
            self.w.show_log("Авторазмещение выполнено")

            result_dlg = AutoPlaceResultDialog(
                result, params, result.threads_used, before_euc, before_man, self.w
            )
            result_dlg.exec()

            if result_dlg.get_code() == AutoPlaceResultDialog.RESULT_REPEAT:
                dlg = AutoPlaceDialog(self.w, self._last_params, self._last_threads)
                continue
            return

    def on_reset_placement(self):
        if not self.w.board.modules:
            QMessageBox.information(self.w, "Информация", "На плате нет модулей.")
            return

        for module in self.w.board.modules:
            module.x = 0.0
            module.y = 0.0
            module.angle = 0.0
            item = self.w.manager.items.get(module.uid)
            if item:
                item.setPos(0.0, 0.0)
                item.setRotation(0.0)
                item.prepareGeometryChange()
                item.update()

        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.w.commit_snapshot()
        self.w.show_log("Размещение сброшено: все элементы в (0, 0)").
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\ui\commands\drawing.py

import copy
from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QGraphicsItem

from constants.board_settings import DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH
from common.ui.dialogs.arc_dialog import ArcDialog
from common.ui.dialogs.text_dialog import TextDialog
from pbe_editor.core.board_models import (
    BoardModule, BoardLine, BoardCircle, BoardArc, BoardText, PadNet,
    prefix_from_footprint_name,
)
from pbe_editor.ui.panels.drawing_tool_bar import BoardTool


class DrawingCommandsMixin:
    def on_canvas_right_clicked(self):
        self.w.exit_to_select_mode()

    def on_canvas_clicked(self, x, y):
        tool = self.w.current_tool
        if tool == BoardTool.ADD_LINE:
            self._add_line_click(x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_CIRCLE:
            self._add_circle_click(x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_ARC:
            self._add_arc_click(x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_TEXT:
            self._add_text_click(x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_MODULE:
            self._add_module_click(x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_NET:
            self._add_net_click(x, y)

    def on_mouse_moved(self, x, y):
        self._current_mouse_pos = QPointF(x, y)
        self.w.coords_label.setText(f"X: {x:.4f}, Y: {y:.4f}")
        tool = self.w.current_tool

        if tool == BoardTool.ADD_NET:
            tol = self.w.canvas.grid_size * 1.5
            hit = self.w.board.find_pad_at(x, y, tol)
            if hit is not None:
                module, pad_number = hit
                pos = module.get_pad_absolute(pad_number)
                pad = None
                for p in module.footprint.pads:
                    if p.number == pad_number:
                        pad = p
                        break
                if pos and pad:
                    radius = max(pad.width, pad.height) * 0.8
                    self.w.canvas.set_highlight_pad(QPointF(*pos), radius)
                else:
                    self.w.canvas.clear_highlight_pad()
            else:
                self.w.canvas.clear_highlight_pad()

            if self.w._net_start:
                sx, sy = self.w._net_start[2]
                self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(sx, sy, x, y)
            return

        self.w.canvas.clear_highlight_pad()

        if tool == BoardTool.ADD_LINE and self.w._line_start:
            sx, sy = self.w._line_start
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(sx, sy, x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_CIRCLE and self.w._circle_center_point:
            cx, cy = self.w._circle_center_point
            g = self.w.canvas.grid_size
            r = round(((x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2) ** 0.5 / g) * g
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_circle(cx, cy, r)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_ARC and self.w._arc_center_point and self.w._arc_start_point is None:
            cx, cy = self.w._arc_center_point
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(cx, cy, x, y)
        elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_TEXT and self.w._text_position:
            tx, ty = self.w._text_position
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(tx, ty, "", 1.0, True)
        else:
            self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_line()
            self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_circle()

    def _add_graphic_item(self, model, kind):
        if kind == "line":
            self.w.board.lines.append(model)
            item = self.w.manager.add_line(model)
        elif kind == "circle":
            self.w.board.circles.append(model)
            item = self.w.manager.add_circle(model)
        elif kind == "arc":
            self.w.board.arcs.append(model)
            item = self.w.manager.add_arc(model)
        elif kind == "text":
            self.w.board.texts.append(model)
            item = self.w.manager.add_text(model)
        else:
            return None
        if self.w.current_tool != BoardTool.SELECT:
            try:
                item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, False)
                item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, False)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        return item

    def _add_line_click(self, x, y):
        if self.w._line_start is None:
            self.w._line_start = (x, y)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)
            return
        sx, sy = self.w._line_start
        if abs(sx - x) < 1e-6 and abs(sy - y) < 1e-6:
            return
        line = BoardLine(
            x1=sx, y1=sy, x2=x, y2=y,
            layer=self.w._line_layer, width=DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH,
            uid=self.w.next_line_uid(),
        )
        self._add_graphic_item(line, "line")
        self.w._line_start = (x, y)
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)
        self.w.commit_snapshot()
        self.w.show_log(f"Линия {self.w._line_layer}: добавлена")

    def _add_circle_click(self, x, y):
        g = self.w.canvas.grid_size
        if self.w._circle_center_point is None:
            self.w._circle_center_point = (x, y)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_circle(x, y, 0.0)
            return
        cx, cy = self.w._circle_center_point
        r = round(((x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2) ** 0.5 / g) * g
        if r > 0:
            circle = BoardCircle(
                x=cx, y=cy, radius=r,
                layer=self.w._line_layer, width=DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH,
                uid=self.w.next_circle_uid(),
            )
            self._add_graphic_item(circle, "circle")
            self.w.commit_snapshot()
            self.w.show_log(f"Окружность {self.w._line_layer}: добавлена")
        self.w._circle_center_point = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_circle()

    def _add_arc_click(self, x, y):
        if self.w._arc_center_point is None:
            self.w._arc_center_point = (x, y)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)
            return
        if self.w._arc_start_point is None:
            self.w._arc_start_point = (x, y)
            cx, cy = self.w._arc_center_point
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(cx, cy, x, y)
            self._show_arc_dialog()
            return
        self.w._arc_center_point = (x, y)
        self.w._arc_start_point = None
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)

    def _show_arc_dialog(self):
        cx, cy = self.w._arc_center_point
        sx, sy = self.w._arc_start_point

        dlg = ArcDialog(self.w)
        dlg.angle_changed.connect(
            lambda a: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_arc(cx, cy, sx, sy, a)
        )
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_arc(cx, cy, sx, sy, dlg.get_angle())

        if dlg.exec() == QDialog.Accepted:
            arc = BoardArc(
                x=cx, y=cy, sx=sx, sy=sy,
                angle=dlg.get_angle(),
                layer=self.w._line_layer, width=DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH,
                uid=self.w.next_arc_uid(),
            )
            self._add_graphic_item(arc, "arc")
            self.w.commit_snapshot()
            self.w.show_log(f"Дуга {self.w._line_layer}: добавлена")

        self.w._arc_center_point = None
        self.w._arc_start_point = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_arc()

    def _add_text_click(self, x, y):
        self.w._text_position = (x, y)
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(x, y, "", 1.0, True)

        dialog = TextDialog(self.w)
        dialog.text_changed.connect(
            lambda t: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(
                self.w._text_position[0], self.w._text_position[1],
                t, dialog.get_size(), dialog.get_visible()))
        dialog.size_changed.connect(
            lambda s: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(
                self.w._text_position[0], self.w._text_position[1],
                dialog.get_text(), s, dialog.get_visible()))
        dialog.visible_changed.connect(
            lambda v: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(
                self.w._text_position[0], self.w._text_position[1],
                dialog.get_text(), dialog.get_size(), v))

        if dialog.exec() == QDialog.Accepted:
            text_value = dialog.get_text() or "Text"
            tx, ty = self.w._text_position
            text = BoardText(
                x=tx, y=ty, text=text_value,
                size=dialog.get_size(), visible=dialog.get_visible(),
                layer=self.w._line_layer, uid=self.w.next_text_uid(),
            )
            self._add_graphic_item(text, "text")
            self.w.commit_snapshot()
            self.w.show_log(f"Текст {self.w._line_layer}: добавлен")

        self.w._text_position = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_text()

    def _add_module_click(self, x, y):
        fp = self.w._selected_footprint
        if fp is None:
            self.w.show_log("Сначала выберите модуль справа")
            return
        prefix = prefix_from_footprint_name(fp.name)
        module = BoardModule(
            footprint=copy.deepcopy(fp),
            reference=self.w.board.auto_reference(prefix),
            value=fp.value,
            x=x, y=y, angle=0.0, layer="F.Cu",
            uid=self.w.next_module_uid(),
        )
        self.w.board.modules.append(module)
        item = self.w.manager.add_module(module)
        if self.w.current_tool != BoardTool.SELECT:
            try:
                item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, False)
                item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, False)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.w.refresh_module_list()
        self.w.commit_snapshot()
        self.w.show_log(f"Модуль {module.reference} добавлен")

    def _add_net_click(self, x, y):
        tol = self.w.canvas.grid_size * 1.5
        hit = self.w.board.find_pad_at(x, y, tol)
        if hit is None:
            self.w.show_log("Не найден вывод в точке")
            return
        module, pad_number = hit
        pos = module.get_pad_absolute(pad_number)
        if self.w._net_start is None:
            self.w._net_start = (module, pad_number, pos)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(pos[0], pos[1], pos[0], pos[1])
            return
        m1, p1, pos1 = self.w._net_start
        if (m1, p1) == (module, pad_number):
            self.w._net_start = None
            self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_line()
            return
        pn1 = m1.pad_nets.get(p1)
        pn2 = module.pad_nets.get(pad_number)
        net_name = None
        if pn1 and pn1.net_name:
            net_name = pn1.net_name
        elif pn2 and pn2.net_name:
            net_name = pn2.net_name
        if net_name is None:
            net_name = f"Net-{m1.reference}-{p1}-{module.reference}-{pad_number}"
        net = self.w.board.get_or_create_net(net_name)
        for m, p in ((m1, p1), (module, pad_number)):
            m.pad_nets[p] = PadNet(pad_number=p, net_code=net.code, net_name=net.name)
        self.w._net_start = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_line()
        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.w.commit_snapshot()
        self.w.show_log(f"Связь {net_name}: {m1.reference}.{p1} ↔ {module.reference}.{pad_number}").
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\ui\commands\editing.py
============================================================
import copy

from pbe_editor.core.board_models import (
    BoardModule, BoardLine, BoardCircle, BoardArc, BoardText, PadNet,
    prefix_from_footprint_name,
)
from pbe_editor.graphics.module_item import BoardModuleGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.graphics.board_line_item import (
    BoardLineGraphicsItem, BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
    BoardTextGraphicsItem,
)
from pbe_editor.graphics.ratsnest_item import RatsnestGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.ui.commands._shared import (
    model_center, translate_model, rotate_model_90, next_uid,
)


class EditingCommandsMixin:
    def delete_selected(self):
        deleted_modules = []
        deleted_lines = []
        deleted_circles = []
        deleted_arcs = []
        deleted_texts = []
        deleted_ratsnest = []
        for item in list(self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems()):
            if isinstance(item, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                deleted_modules.append(item.module)
            elif isinstance(item, BoardLineGraphicsItem):
                deleted_lines.append(item.model)
            elif isinstance(item, BoardCircleGraphicsItem):
                deleted_circles.append(item.model)
            elif isinstance(item, BoardArcGraphicsItem):
                deleted_arcs.append(item.model)
            elif isinstance(item, BoardTextGraphicsItem):
                deleted_texts.append(item.model)
            elif isinstance(item, RatsnestGraphicsItem):
                deleted_ratsnest.append(item)

        deleted_net_codes = set()
        for r in deleted_ratsnest:
            if r.net_code > 0:
                deleted_net_codes.add(r.net_code)

        for m in deleted_modules:
            self.w.manager.remove_module(m)
            if m in self.w.board.modules:
                self.w.board.modules.remove(m)
        for l in deleted_lines:
            self.w.manager.remove_line(l)
            if l in self.w.board.lines:
                self.w.board.lines.remove(l)
        for c in deleted_circles:
            self.w.manager.remove_circle(c)
            if c in self.w.board.circles:
                self.w.board.circles.remove(c)
        for a in deleted_arcs:
            self.w.manager.remove_arc(a)
            if a in self.w.board.arcs:
                self.w.board.arcs.remove(a)
        for t in deleted_texts:
            self.w.manager.remove_text(t)
            if t in self.w.board.texts:
                self.w.board.texts.remove(t)
        for r in deleted_ratsnest:
            if r in self.w.manager.ratsnest_items:
                self.w.manager.ratsnest_items.remove(r)
            try:
                self.w.canvas.scene.removeItem(r)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        if deleted_net_codes:
            for module in self.w.board.modules:
                for pad_number in list(module.pad_nets.keys()):
                    if module.pad_nets[pad_number].net_code in deleted_net_codes:
                        del module.pad_nets[pad_number]

        if deleted_modules or deleted_lines or deleted_circles or deleted_arcs or deleted_texts or deleted_ratsnest:
            self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
            self.w.refresh_module_list()
            self.w.commit_snapshot()
            self.w.show_log(
                f"Удалено: {len(deleted_modules)} модулей, "
                f"{len(deleted_lines)} линий, {len(deleted_circles)} окружностей, "
                f"{len(deleted_arcs)} дуг, {len(deleted_texts)} текстов, "
                f"{len(deleted_ratsnest)} связей"
            )

    def rotate_selected(self):
        items = [it for it in self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems()
                 if isinstance(it, (BoardModuleGraphicsItem, BoardLineGraphicsItem,
                                    BoardArcGraphicsItem))]
        if not items:
            return
        for item in items:
            if isinstance(item, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                rotate_model_90(item.module)
                item.setRotation(item.module.angle)
                item.prepareGeometryChange()
                item.update()
            elif isinstance(item, BoardLineGraphicsItem):
                rotate_model_90(item.model)
                item.setPos(item.model.x1, item.model.y1)
                item.prepareGeometryChange()
                item.update()
            elif isinstance(item, BoardArcGraphicsItem):
                rotate_model_90(item.model)
                item.setPos(item.model.x, item.model.y)
                item.prepareGeometryChange()
                item.update()
        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.w.commit_snapshot()
        self.w.show_log(f"Повёрнуто: {len(items)} элементов")

    def copy_selected(self):
        self._clipboard = []
        for item in self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems():
            if isinstance(item, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                self._clipboard.append(copy.deepcopy(item.module))
            elif isinstance(item, BoardLineGraphicsItem):
                self._clipboard.append(copy.deepcopy(item.model))
            elif isinstance(item, BoardCircleGraphicsItem):
                self._clipboard.append(copy.deepcopy(item.model))
            elif isinstance(item, BoardArcGraphicsItem):
                self._clipboard.append(copy.deepcopy(item.model))
            elif isinstance(item, BoardTextGraphicsItem):
                self._clipboard.append(copy.deepcopy(item.model))
        if self._clipboard:
            self.w.show_log(f"Скопировано: {len(self._clipboard)} элементов")

    def _clipboard_center(self):
        if not self._clipboard:
            return 0.0, 0.0
        sx = sy = 0.0
        n = 0
        for m in self._clipboard:
            px, py = model_center(m)
            sx += px
            sy += py
            n += 1
        return (sx / n, sy / n) if n else (0.0, 0.0)

    def paste(self):
        if not self._clipboard:
            return
        self.w.canvas.scene.clearSelection()
        cx, cy = self._clipboard_center()
        dx = self._current_mouse_pos.x() - cx
        dy = self._current_mouse_pos.y() - cy
        grid = self.w.canvas.grid_size
        dx = round(dx / grid) * grid
        dy = round(dy / grid) * grid

        for model in self._clipboard:
            new_model = copy.deepcopy(model)
            if isinstance(new_model, BoardModule):
                new_model.uid = next_uid(self.w.board.modules)
                new_model.reference = self.w.board.auto_reference(
                    prefix_from_footprint_name(new_model.footprint.name))
                translate_model(new_model, dx, dy)
                self.w.board.modules.append(new_model)
                item = self.w.manager.add_module(new_model)
            elif isinstance(new_model, BoardLine):
                new_model.uid = next_uid(self.w.board.lines)
                translate_model(new_model, dx, dy)
                self.w.board.lines.append(new_model)
                item = self.w.manager.add_line(new_model)
            elif isinstance(new_model, BoardCircle):
                new_model.uid = next_uid(self.w.board.circles)
                translate_model(new_model, dx, dy)
                self.w.board.circles.append(new_model)
                item = self.w.manager.add_circle(new_model)
            elif isinstance(new_model, BoardArc):
                new_model.uid = next_uid(self.w.board.arcs)
                translate_model(new_model, dx, dy)
                self.w.board.arcs.append(new_model)
                item = self.w.manager.add_arc(new_model)
            elif isinstance(new_model, BoardText):
                new_model.uid = next_uid(self.w.board.texts)
                translate_model(new_model, dx, dy)
                self.w.board.texts.append(new_model)
                item = self.w.manager.add_text(new_model)
            else:
                continue
            item.setSelected(True)

        self.w.refresh_module_list()
        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.w.commit_snapshot()
        self.w.show_log(f"Вставлено: {len(self._clipboard)} элементов").
.

from pbe_editor.graphics.module_item import BoardModuleGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.graphics.board_line_item import (
    BoardLineGraphicsItem, BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
    BoardTextGraphicsItem,
)
from pbe_editor.graphics.ratsnest_item import RatsnestGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.ui.panels.drawing_tool_bar import BoardTool


class SelectionCommandsMixin:
    def on_selection_changed(self):
        if self.w.current_tool != BoardTool.SELECT:
            return
        items = self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems()
        self.w.aux_bar.set_delete_enabled(len(items) > 0)
        if len(items) == 1:
            item = items[0]
            if isinstance(item, (BoardModuleGraphicsItem, BoardLineGraphicsItem,
                                 BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
                                 BoardTextGraphicsItem, RatsnestGraphicsItem)):
                self.w.right_panel.properties_panel.show_object(item)
                return
        self.w.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()

    def on_apply_properties(self):
        self.w.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.w.refresh_module_list()
        self.w.commit_snapshot()

    def on_line_layer_selected(self, layer):
        self.w._line_layer = layer
        self.w.show_log(f"Слой рисования: {layer}")

    def on_module_picked_from_panel(self, fp):
        self.w._selected_footprint = fp
        self.w.show_log(f"Выбран модуль для вставки: {fp.name}")

    def on_module_list_clicked(self, list_item):
        idx = self.w.module_list.row(list_item)
        if 0 <= idx < len(self.w.board.modules):
            module = self.w.board.modules[idx]
            item = self.w.manager.items.get(module.uid)
            if item is not None:
                self.w.canvas.scene.clearSelection()
                item.setSelected(True)
                self.w.right_panel.properties_panel.show_object(item)
                self.w.canvas.centerOn(item).
.

from pbe_editor.core.board_models import (
    BoardModule, BoardLine, BoardCircle, BoardArc, BoardText,
)
from pbe_editor.core.board_models import prefix_from_footprint_name


def model_center(m):
    if isinstance(m, BoardModule):
        return m.x, m.y
    if isinstance(m, BoardLine):
        return (m.x1 + m.x2) / 2.0, (m.y1 + m.y2) / 2.0
    if isinstance(m, (BoardCircle, BoardArc, BoardText)):
        return m.x, m.y
    return 0.0, 0.0


def translate_model(m, dx, dy):
    if isinstance(m, BoardModule):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy
    elif isinstance(m, BoardLine):
        m.x1 += dx
        m.y1 += dy
        m.x2 += dx
        m.y2 += dy
    elif isinstance(m, BoardArc):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy
        m.sx += dx
        m.sy += dy
    elif isinstance(m, (BoardCircle, BoardText)):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy


def rotate_model_90(m):
    if isinstance(m, BoardModule):
        m.angle = (m.angle + 90.0) % 360.0
    elif isinstance(m, BoardLine):
        cx = (m.x1 + m.x2) / 2.0
        cy = (m.y1 + m.y2) / 2.0
        dx1, dy1 = m.x1 - cx, m.y1 - cy
        dx2, dy2 = m.x2 - cx, m.y2 - cy
        m.x1, m.y1 = cx - dy1, cy + dx1
        m.x2, m.y2 = cx - dy2, cy + dx2
    elif isinstance(m, BoardArc):
        dx, dy = m.sx - m.x, m.sy - m.y
        m.sx = m.x - dy
        m.sy = m.y + dx


def next_uid(items):
    if not items:
        return 1
    return max(x.uid for x in items) + 1


def auto_reference_for(board, fp_name):
    return board.auto_reference(prefix_from_footprint_name(fp_name)).
.

LAYER_LIST_QSS = (
    "QListView { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
    " border: 1px solid #555555; outline: 0; }"
    "QListView::item { padding: 4px 8px; }"
    "QListView::item:hover { background-color: #3c3f41; }"
    "QListView::item:selected { background-color: #365880; color: #ffffff; }"
)


def _styled_combo(items):
    combo = QComboBox()
    for label, data in items:
        combo.addItem(label, data)
    view = QListView()
    view.setStyleSheet(LAYER_LIST_QSS)
    combo.setView(view)
    combo.setStyleSheet(inline_combo_qss())
    combo.setMinimumHeight(24)
    return combo

        idx = self.cb_opt_mode.findData(p.optimization_mode)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.cb_opt_mode.setCurrentIndex(idx)

    def get_params(self) -> PlacementParams:
        return PlacementParams(
            A=self.sb_A.value(),
            B=self.sb_B.value(),
            C=self.sb_C.value(),
            metric=self.cb_metric.currentData(),
            j_method=self.cb_j.currentData(),
            directive_mode=self.cb_mode.currentData(),
            directive_position=self.cb_pos.currentData(),
            optimization_mode=self.cb_opt_mode.currentData(),
        )

    def get_threads(self) -> int:
        return self.sb_threads.value().

J_METHOD_LABELS = {
    JMethod.M_331: "3.3.1 (Макс. связь)",
    JMethod.M_333: "3.3.3 (Сумма связей)",
    JMethod.M_335: "3.3.5 (Разность связей)",
    JMethod.M_336: "3.3.6 (Отн. связность)",
}

OPT_MODE_LABELS = {

    OptimizationMode.D_C: "d = C",
    OptimizationMode.D_C2: "d = C/2",
    OptimizationMode.D_C4: "d = C/4",

class AutoPlaceResultDialog(HeaderlessDialog):
    RESULT_OK = 1
    RESULT_REPEAT = 2

    def __init__(self, result, params, threads,
                 initial_wire_euc, initial_wire_man, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent, title="Результаты авторазмещения")
        has_opt = params.optimization_mode != OptimizationMode.NONE
        self.setMinimumSize(720 if has_opt else 620, 580)

        self._result = result
        self._params = params
        self._threads = threads
        self._code = self.RESULT_OK

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        header = QLabel("Результаты авторазмещения")

        layout.addWidget(header)

        metric_text = "Евклидова" if params.metric.value == "euclidean" else "Ортогональная"
        mode_text = "Самый крупный" if params.directive_mode.value == "largest" else "Самый связанный"
        pos_text = {
            "center": "Центр",

            "bottom_left": "Левый нижний",
        }.get(params.directive_position.value, "-")

        method_lbl = QLabel(

        )
        fix_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")

        self.btn_repeat.clicked.connect(self._on_repeat)

        self.btn_ok = QPushButton("OK")
        self.btn_ok.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_OK)
        self.btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)

        btns.addWidget(self.btn_repeat)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_ok)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

    def _pct_cell(self, before, after):
        if before <= 0:
            return f"{after:.2f}"
        pct = (after - before) / before * 100
        return f"{after:.2f} ({pct:+.1f} %)"

    def _on_repeat(self):
        self._code = self.RESULT_REPEAT
        self.accept()

    def get_code(self):
        return self._code.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QTableWidget,
                               QTableWidgetItem, QPushButton, QHeaderView, QLabel)
from constants import Styles
from common.ui.dialogs.headerless_dialog import HeaderlessDialog


class NetsDialog(HeaderlessDialog):
    def __init__(self, board, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent, title="Связи на плате")
        self.resize(700, 450)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        header = QLabel("Связи (nets)")
        header.setStyleSheet(Styles.OBJECT_TITLE)
        layout.addWidget(header)

        self.table = QTableWidget(0, 3)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Код", "Имя цепи", "Пины"])
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self.table.setStyleSheet(
            "QTableWidget { background-color: #1e1e1e; color: #cfcfcf;"
            " gridline-color: #3c3f41; border: 1px solid #3c3f41; }"
            "QHeaderView::section { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
            " border: 0px; border-right: 1px solid #3c3f41; padding: 4px; }"
        )
        layout.addWidget(self.table)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        btns.addStretch()
        self.btn_close = QPushButton("Закрыть")
        self.btn_close.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_close)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

        self._fill(board)

    def _fill(self, board):
        pin_index = {}
        for module in board.modules:
            for pad_number, pad_net in module.pad_nets.items():
                key = pad_net.net_code
                label = f"{module.reference}.{pad_number}"
                pin_index.setdefault(key, []).append(label)

        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        for code in sorted(board.nets.keys()):
            net = board.nets[code]
            row = self.table.rowCount()
            self.table.insertRow(row)
            self.table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(net.code)))
            self.table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(net.name))
            pins = ", ".join(pin_index.get(net.code, []))
            self.table.setItem(row, 2, QTableWidgetItem(pins)).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QVBoxLayout,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QComboBox,
    QDoubleSpinBox,
    QPushButton,
    QFormLayout,
    QGroupBox,
    QListView,
)
from constants import Styles
from common.ui.dialogs.headerless_dialog import HeaderlessDialog
from common.ui.qss import inline_combo_qss
from pbe_editor.core.autoplacer import Metric, OptimizationMode
from pbe_editor.core.pairwise_placer import (
    PairwiseParams,
    PairwiseAlgoVersion,
    PairwiseDirectiveMode,
)

LAYER_LIST_QSS = (
    "QListView { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
    " border: 1px solid #555555; outline: 0; }"
    "QListView::item { padding: 4px 8px; }"
    "QListView::item:hover { background-color: #3c3f41; }"
    "QListView::item:selected { background-color: #365880; color: #ffffff; }"
)


def _styled_combo(items):
    combo = QComboBox()
    for label, data in items:
        combo.addItem(label, data)
    view = QListView()
    view.setStyleSheet(LAYER_LIST_QSS)
    combo.setView(view)
    combo.setStyleSheet(inline_combo_qss())
    combo.setMinimumHeight(24)
    return combo


class PairwiseDialog(HeaderlessDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, initial_params: PairwiseParams = None):
        super().__init__(parent, title="Авторазмещение (парные перестановки)")
        self.setMinimumWidth(560)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        header = QLabel("Авторазмещение методом парных перестановок")
        header.setStyleSheet(Styles.OBJECT_TITLE)
        layout.addWidget(header)

        columns = QHBoxLayout()
        columns.setSpacing(16)

        left_col = QVBoxLayout()
        right_col = QVBoxLayout()

        group_geom = QGroupBox("Геометрия")
        group_geom.setStyleSheet(Styles.MAIN_WINDOW)
        form_geom = QFormLayout(group_geom)
        form_geom.setSpacing(6)

        self.sb_A = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.sb_A.setRange(0.1, 100.0)
        self.sb_A.setValue(2.0)
        self.sb_A.setSuffix(" мм")
        self.sb_A.setStyleSheet(Styles.INPUT_BG)
        form_geom.addRow("Отступ A:", self.sb_A)

        self.sb_B = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.sb_B.setRange(0.1, 100.0)
        self.sb_B.setValue(2.0)
        self.sb_B.setSuffix(" мм")
        self.sb_B.setStyleSheet(Styles.INPUT_BG)
        form_geom.addRow("Зазор B:", self.sb_B)

        self.sb_C = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.sb_C.setRange(0.1, 10.0)
        self.sb_C.setValue(1.25)
        self.sb_C.setSuffix(" мм")
        self.sb_C.setStyleSheet(Styles.INPUT_BG)
        form_geom.addRow("Шаг сетки C:", self.sb_C)

        left_col.addWidget(group_geom)

        group_algo = QGroupBox("Алгоритм")
        group_algo.setStyleSheet(Styles.MAIN_WINDOW)
        form_algo = QFormLayout(group_algo)
        form_algo.setSpacing(6)

        self.cb_version = _styled_combo(
            [
                ("Сапрыкин (матричный)", PairwiseAlgoVersion.SAPRYKIN),
                ("Селютин (упорядоченный)", PairwiseAlgoVersion.SELYUTIN),
            ]
        )
        form_algo.addRow("Реализация алгоритма:", self.cb_version)

        self.cb_metric = _styled_combo(
            [
                ("Обычная (Евклидова)", Metric.EUCLIDEAN),
                ("Ортогональная", Metric.MANHATTAN),
            ]
        )
        form_algo.addRow("Используемая метрика:", self.cb_metric)

        self.cb_directive = _styled_combo(
            [
                ("Отсутствует", PairwiseDirectiveMode.NONE),
                ("Самый крупный", PairwiseDirectiveMode.LARGEST),
                ("Самый связанный", PairwiseDirectiveMode.MOST_CONNECTED),
            ]
        )
        form_algo.addRow("Директивный элемент:", self.cb_directive)

        self.cb_opt_mode = _styled_combo(
            [
                ("Без оптимизации", OptimizationMode.NONE),
                ("d = C", OptimizationMode.D_C),
                ("d = C/2", OptimizationMode.D_C2),
                ("d = C/4", OptimizationMode.D_C4),
            ]
        )
        self.cb_opt_mode.setCurrentIndex(2)
        form_algo.addRow("Локальная оптимизация:", self.cb_opt_mode)

        right_col.addWidget(group_algo)

        columns.addLayout(left_col, 1)
        columns.addLayout(right_col, 1)
        layout.addLayout(columns)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        btns.addStretch()
        self.btn_ok = QPushButton("Запустить")
        self.btn_ok.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_OK)
        self.btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        self.btn_cancel = QPushButton("Отмена")
        self.btn_cancel.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_ok)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

        if initial_params:
            self.set_params(initial_params)

    def set_params(self, p: PairwiseParams):
        self.sb_A.setValue(p.A)
        self.sb_B.setValue(p.B)
        self.sb_C.setValue(p.C)

        idx = self.cb_version.findData(p.algo_version)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.cb_version.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        idx = self.cb_metric.findData(p.metric)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.cb_metric.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        idx = self.cb_directive.findData(p.directive_mode)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.cb_directive.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        idx = self.cb_opt_mode.findData(p.optimization_mode)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.cb_opt_mode.setCurrentIndex(idx)

    def get_params(self) -> PairwiseParams:
        return PairwiseParams(
            A=self.sb_A.value(),
            B=self.sb_B.value(),
            C=self.sb_C.value(),
            algo_version=self.cb_version.currentData(),
            metric=self.cb_metric.currentData(),
            directive_mode=self.cb_directive.currentData(),
            optimization_mode=self.cb_opt_mode.currentData(),
        ).
.
