from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QVBoxLayout,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QTableWidget,
    QTableWidgetItem,
    QPushButton,
    QHeaderView,
    QWidget,
)
from constants import Styles
from common.ui.dialogs.headerless_dialog import HeaderlessDialog
from common.ui.qss import TABLE_QSS
from pbe_editor.core.autoplacer import OptimizationMode
from pbe_editor.core.pairwise_placer import PairwiseAlgoVersion, PairwiseDirectiveMode


VERSION_LABELS = {
    PairwiseAlgoVersion.SAPRYKIN: "Сапрыкин (матричный)",
    PairwiseAlgoVersion.SELYUTIN: "Селютин (упорядоченный)",
}

DIRECTIVE_LABELS = {
    PairwiseDirectiveMode.NONE: "Отсутствует",
    PairwiseDirectiveMode.LARGEST: "Самый крупный",
    PairwiseDirectiveMode.MOST_CONNECTED: "Самый связанный",
}

OPT_MODE_LABELS = {
    OptimizationMode.NONE: "Без оптимизации",
    OptimizationMode.D_C: "d = C",
    OptimizationMode.D_C2: "d = C/2",
    OptimizationMode.D_C4: "d = C/4",
}


class PairwiseResultDialog(HeaderlessDialog):
    RESULT_OK = 1
    RESULT_REPEAT = 2

    def __init__(self, result, params, initial_wire_euc, initial_wire_man, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent, title="Результаты парных перестановок")
        has_opt = params.optimization_mode != OptimizationMode.NONE
        self.setMinimumSize(720 if has_opt else 620, 580)

        self._result = result
        self._params = params
        self._code = self.RESULT_OK

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        header = QLabel("Результаты авторазмещения (парные перестановки)")
        header.setStyleSheet(Styles.OBJECT_TITLE)
        layout.addWidget(header)

        version_text = VERSION_LABELS.get(params.algo_version, params.algo_version.value)
        metric_text = "Обычная (Евклидова)" if params.metric.value == "euclidean" else "Ортогональная"
        dir_text = DIRECTIVE_LABELS.get(params.directive_mode, params.directive_mode.value)
        opt_text = OPT_MODE_LABELS.get(params.optimization_mode, params.optimization_mode.value)

        board_lbl = QLabel(
            "Ширина платы: <b>{0:.2f} мм</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Длина платы: <b>{1:.2f} мм</b>".format(result.board_width, result.board_length)
        )
        board_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(board_lbl)

        scheme_lbl = QLabel(
            "Корпусов: <b>{0}</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Связей: <b>{1}</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Сдвинуто с границы: <b>{2}</b>".format(
                result.num_footprints, result.num_connections, result.shifted_count
            )
        )
        scheme_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(scheme_lbl)

        geom_lbl = QLabel(
            "Отступ A: <b>{0:.2f} мм</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Зазор B: <b>{1:.2f} мм</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Шаг сетки C: <b>{2:.2f} мм</b>".format(params.A, params.B, params.C)
        )
        geom_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(geom_lbl)

        algo_lbl = QLabel(
            "Реализация: <b>{0}</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Метрика: <b>{1}</b>".format(version_text, metric_text)
        )
        algo_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(algo_lbl)

        fix_lbl = QLabel(
            "Директивный элемент: <b>{0}</b> ({1})&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
            "Локальная оптимизация: <b>{2}</b>".format(
                dir_text, result.directive_ref or "-", opt_text
            )
        )
        fix_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(fix_lbl)

        spacer_top = QWidget()
        spacer_top.setFixedHeight(8)
        layout.addWidget(spacer_top)

        if has_opt:
            headers = ["Показатель", "До размещения", "После перестановок", "После оптимизации"]
        else:
            headers = ["Показатель", "До размещения", "После перестановок"]

        self.table = QTableWidget(0, len(headers))
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        for col in range(1, len(headers)):
            self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(col, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self.table.setStyleSheet(TABLE_QSS)
        layout.addWidget(self.table)

        if has_opt:
            rows = [
                (
                    "Длина по Евклиду, мм",
                    f"{initial_wire_euc:.2f}",
                    self._pct_cell(initial_wire_euc, result.pair_wire_euclidean),
                    self._pct_cell(result.pair_wire_euclidean, result.wire_euclidean),
                ),
                (
                    "Длина по Манхэттену, мм",
                    f"{initial_wire_man:.2f}",
                    self._pct_cell(initial_wire_man, result.pair_wire_manhattan),
                    self._pct_cell(result.pair_wire_manhattan, result.wire_manhattan),
                ),
            ]
        else:
            rows = [
                (
                    "Длина по Евклиду, мм",
                    f"{initial_wire_euc:.2f}",
                    self._pct_cell(initial_wire_euc, result.wire_euclidean),
                ),
                (
                    "Длина по Манхэттену, мм",
                    f"{initial_wire_man:.2f}",
                    self._pct_cell(initial_wire_man, result.wire_manhattan),
                ),
            ]

        self.table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            for j, val in enumerate(row):
                self.table.setItem(i, j, QTableWidgetItem(val))

        spacer_bottom = QWidget()
        spacer_bottom.setFixedHeight(6)
        layout.addWidget(spacer_bottom)

        pair_time_lbl = QLabel(
            "Время парных перестановок: <b>{0:.1f} мс</b>".format(result.pair_time_seconds * 1000)
        )
        pair_time_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(pair_time_lbl)

        opt_time_lbl = QLabel(
            "Время локальной оптимизации: <b>{0:.1f} мс</b>".format(result.opt_time_seconds * 1000)
        )
        opt_time_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(opt_time_lbl)

        total_time_lbl = QLabel(
            "Общее время: <b>{0:.1f} мс</b>".format(result.time_seconds * 1000)
        )
        total_time_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a9b7c6;")
        layout.addWidget(total_time_lbl)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        btns.addStretch()

        self.btn_repeat = QPushButton("Повторить")
        self.btn_repeat.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_repeat.clicked.connect(self._on_repeat)

        self.btn_ok = QPushButton("OK")
        self.btn_ok.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_OK)
        self.btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)

        btns.addWidget(self.btn_repeat)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_ok)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

    def _pct_cell(self, before, after):
        if before <= 0:
            return f"{after:.2f}"
        pct = (after - before) / before * 100
        return f"{after:.2f} ({pct:+.1f} %)"

    def _on_repeat(self):
        self._code = self.RESULT_REPEAT
        self.accept()

    def get_code(self):
        return self._code.
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\ui\panels\board_library_panel.py

from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                               QPushButton, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from constants import Styles
from pbe_editor.ui.dialogs.libraries_dialog import LibrariesManagerDialog


PANEL_MIN_WIDTH = 160
PANEL_MAX_WIDTH = 320

ADD_BUTTON_STYLE = (
    "QPushButton { background-color: #365880; color: white;"
    " font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold;"
    " border: none; padding: 0px; }"
    " QPushButton:hover { background-color: #2a4a6b; }"
)


class BoardLibraryPanel(QWidget):
    add_library_requested = Signal()
    library_selected = Signal(str)
    module_add_requested = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setMinimumWidth(PANEL_MIN_WIDTH)
        self.setMaximumWidth(PANEL_MAX_WIDTH)
        self._paths = []
        self._modules = {}

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(6, 6, 6, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        header = QHBoxLayout()
        header.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        title = QLabel("Библиотеки")
        title.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        header.addWidget(title)
        header.addStretch()
        self.btn_manage = QPushButton("📚")
        self.btn_manage.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        self.btn_manage.setToolTip("Подключенные библиотеки")
        self.btn_manage.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        self.btn_manage.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_manage.clicked.connect(self._on_manage_clicked)
        header.addWidget(self.btn_manage)
        self.btn_add_library = QPushButton("+")
        self.btn_add_library.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        self.btn_add_library.setToolTip("Добавить библиотеку")
        self.btn_add_library.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        self.btn_add_library.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_add_library.clicked.connect(self.add_library_requested.emit)
        header.addWidget(self.btn_add_library)
        layout.addLayout(header)

        self.library_list_widget = QListWidget()
        self.library_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.library_list_widget.itemClicked.connect(self._on_library_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.library_list_widget, 1)

        header2 = QHBoxLayout()
        header2.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        title2 = QLabel("Корпуса")
        title2.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        header2.addWidget(title2)
        header2.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(header2)

        self.module_list_widget = QListWidget()
        self.module_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.module_list_widget.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self._on_module_double_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.module_list_widget, 1)

    def _on_manage_clicked(self):
        dlg = LibrariesManagerDialog(self._paths, self)
        if dlg.exec() == LibrariesManagerDialog.Accepted:
            self._paths = dlg.get_library_paths()
            self.add_library_requested.emit()

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.btn_add_library.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        self.btn_manage.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        self.library_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.module_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)

    def _on_library_clicked(self, item):
        path = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if path:
            self.library_selected.emit(path)

    def _on_module_double_clicked(self, item):
        name = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if name:
            self.module_add_requested.emit(name)

    def set_libraries(self, paths):
        self._paths = list(paths)
        self.library_list_widget.clear()
        for path in paths:
            item = QListWidgetItem(Path(path).stem)
            item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, path)
            item.setToolTip(path)
            self.library_list_widget.addItem(item)

    def set_modules(self, names):
        self.module_list_widget.clear()
        for name in sorted(names):
            item = QListWidgetItem(name)
            item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, name)
            item.setToolTip(name)
            self.module_list_widget.addItem(item)

    def highlight_library(self, path):
        if not path:
            self.library_list_widget.clearSelection()
            return
        for i in range(self.library_list_widget.count()):
            item = self.library_list_widget.item(i)
            if item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) == path:
                self.library_list_widget.setCurrentItem(item)
                return

    def highlight_module(self, name):
        if not name:
            self.module_list_widget.clearSelection()
            return
        for i in range(self.module_list_widget.count()):
            item = self.module_list_widget.item(i)
            if item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) == name:
                self.module_list_widget.setCurrentItem(item)
                return.
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QFormLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                               QLineEdit, QComboBox, QListView,
                               QPushButton, QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView,
                               QListWidget, QListWidgetItem, QTreeWidget, QTreeWidgetItem,
                               QCheckBox, QAbstractItemView, QSizePolicy)
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QPixmap, QIcon
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal

from constants import Styles
from constants.board_settings import DRAWABLE_LAYERS, LAYER_NAMES_RU
from common.ui.qss import (
    CHECKBOX_QSS, COMBO_VIEW_QSS, TABLE_QSS,
    form_widget_qss, inline_combo_qss,
)
from common.ui.widgets.numeric_line_edit import NumericLineEdit
from pbe_editor.graphics.module_item import BoardModuleGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.graphics.board_line_item import (
    BoardLineGraphicsItem, BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
    BoardTextGraphicsItem,
)
from pbe_editor.graphics.ratsnest_item import RatsnestGraphicsItem


def _layer_label(name):
    ru = LAYER_NAMES_RU.get(name)
    return f"{name} ({ru})" if ru else name


class BoardPropertiesPanel(QWidget):
    line_layer_selected = Signal(str)
    module_selected = Signal(object)

    MODE_OBJECT = "object"
    MODE_LINE_LAYER = "line_layer"
    MODE_MODULE_PICKER = "module_picker"

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(6)
        self.main_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        self.header_label = QLabel("")
        self.header_label.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        self.header_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)

        self.form_widget = QWidget()
        self.form_widget.setObjectName("PropertiesTable")
        self.form_widget.setStyleSheet(form_widget_qss())
        self.form_widget.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                                       QSizePolicy.Policy.Preferred)
        self.form_layout = QFormLayout(self.form_widget)
        self.form_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 8)
        self.form_layout.setLabelAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
        self.form_layout.setFormAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)
        self.form_layout.setFieldGrowthPolicy(QFormLayout.FieldGrowthPolicy.AllNonFixedFieldsGrow)
        self.form_layout.setRowWrapPolicy(QFormLayout.RowWrapPolicy.DontWrapRows)
        self.form_layout.setHorizontalSpacing(8)
        self.form_layout.setVerticalSpacing(4)

        self.pins_label = QLabel("Связанные пины:")
        self.pins_label.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #a9b7c6;")
        self.pins_table = QTableWidget(0, 3)
        self.pins_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Пин", "Цепь", "Код"])
        self.pins_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        self.pins_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.pins_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        self.pins_table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        self.pins_table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.pins_table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self.pins_table.setStyleSheet(TABLE_QSS)
        self.pins_table.setMinimumHeight(140)
        self.pins_label.hide()
        self.pins_table.hide()

        self.buttons_widget = QWidget()
        btns = QHBoxLayout(self.buttons_widget)
        btns.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        btns.setSpacing(6)
        self.btn_reset = QPushButton("Отменить")
        self.btn_reset.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_reset.clicked.connect(self._reset_changes)
        self.btn_apply = QPushButton("Применить")
        self.btn_apply.setStyleSheet(Styles.APPLY_BUTTON)
        self.btn_apply.clicked.connect(self._apply_changes)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_reset)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_apply)

        self.line_layers_widget = QWidget()
        ll_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.line_layers_widget)
        ll_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        ll_layout.setSpacing(4)
        self.line_layers_list = QListWidget()
        self.line_layers_list.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.line_layers_list.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.SelectionMode.SingleSelection)
        self.line_layers_list.itemClicked.connect(self._on_layer_clicked)
        ll_layout.addWidget(self.line_layers_list)

        self.module_picker_widget = QWidget()
        mp_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.module_picker_widget)
        mp_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        mp_layout.setSpacing(4)
        self.module_tree = QTreeWidget()
        self.module_tree.setHeaderHidden(True)
        self.module_tree.setStyleSheet(
            "QTreeWidget { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
            " border: 1px solid #555555; }"
            "QTreeWidget::item:selected { background-color: #365880; color: white; }"
            "QTreeWidget::item:hover { background-color: #3c3f41; }"
        )
        self.module_tree.itemClicked.connect(self._on_module_tree_clicked)
        mp_layout.addWidget(self.module_tree)
        self.libraries_for_picker = {}

        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.header_label)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.form_widget)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.pins_label)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.pins_table)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.line_layers_widget)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.module_picker_widget)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.buttons_widget)
        self.main_layout.addStretch()

        self.header_label.hide()
        self.form_widget.hide()
        self.pins_label.hide()
        self.pins_table.hide()
        self.buttons_widget.hide()
        self.line_layers_widget.hide()
        self.module_picker_widget.hide()

        self.current_object = None
        self._widgets = {}
        self._building = False
        self._mode = self.MODE_OBJECT

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.header_label.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        self.form_widget.setStyleSheet(form_widget_qss())
        self.btn_apply.setStyleSheet(Styles.APPLY_BUTTON)
        self.btn_reset.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.line_layers_list.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.module_tree.setStyleSheet(
            "QTreeWidget { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
            " border: 1px solid #555555; }"
            "QTreeWidget::item:selected { background-color: #365880; color: white; }"
            "QTreeWidget::item:hover { background-color: #3c3f41; }"
        )

    def show_line_layer_picker(self, current_layer):
        self._set_mode(self.MODE_LINE_LAYER)
        self.header_label.setText("Слой линии")
        self.header_label.show()
        self.line_layers_list.clear()
        selected_item = None
        for name, color in DRAWABLE_LAYERS:
            item = QListWidgetItem(_layer_label(name))
            item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, name)
            pix = QPixmap(14, 14)
            pix.fill(QColor(*color))
            item.setIcon(QIcon(pix))
            self.line_layers_list.addItem(item)
            if name == current_layer:
                selected_item = item
        if selected_item is not None:
            self.line_layers_list.setCurrentItem(selected_item)
            self.line_layers_list.scrollToItem(selected_item)
        self.line_layers_widget.show()
        self.form_widget.hide()
        self.pins_label.hide()
        self.pins_table.hide()
        self.buttons_widget.hide()
        self.module_picker_widget.hide()

    def show_module_picker(self, libraries):
        self._set_mode(self.MODE_MODULE_PICKER)
        self.header_label.setText("Добавить модуль")
        self.header_label.show()
        self.libraries_for_picker = libraries
        self.module_tree.clear()
        for lib_path, modules in libraries.items():
            lib_item = QTreeWidgetItem([lib_path])
            lib_item.setData(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, ("lib", None))
            self.module_tree.addTopLevelItem(lib_item)
            for name, fp in sorted(modules.items()):
                child = QTreeWidgetItem([name])
                child.setData(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, ("module", fp))
                lib_item.addChild(child)
        self.module_tree.expandAll()
        self.module_picker_widget.show()
        self.form_widget.hide()
        self.pins_label.hide()
        self.pins_table.hide()
        self.buttons_widget.hide()
        self.line_layers_widget.hide()

    def show_object(self, obj):
        self._set_mode(self.MODE_OBJECT)
        self.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        try:
            self._building = True
            try:
                while self.form_layout.rowCount() > 0:
                    self.form_layout.removeRow(0)
                self._widgets.clear()
                self.pins_table.setRowCount(0)
            finally:
                self._building = False
            self.current_object = obj
            if isinstance(obj, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                self.header_label.setText(f"Модуль {obj.module.reference}")
                self._build_module_ui(obj.module)
                self.pins_label.show()
                self.pins_table.show()
                self.buttons_widget.show()
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardLineGraphicsItem):
                self.header_label.setText(f"Линия {obj.model.layer}")
                self._build_line_ui(obj.model)
                self.pins_label.hide()
                self.pins_table.hide()
                self.buttons_widget.show()
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardCircleGraphicsItem):
                self.header_label.setText(f"Окружность {obj.model.layer}")
                self._build_circle_ui(obj.model)
                self.pins_label.hide()
                self.pins_table.hide()
                self.buttons_widget.show()
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardArcGraphicsItem):
                self.header_label.setText(f"Дуга {obj.model.layer}")
                self._build_arc_ui(obj.model)
                self.pins_label.hide()
                self.pins_table.hide()
                self.buttons_widget.show()
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardTextGraphicsItem):
                self.header_label.setText(f"Текст {obj.model.layer}")
                self._build_text_ui(obj.model)
                self.pins_label.hide()
                self.pins_table.hide()
                self.buttons_widget.show()
            elif isinstance(obj, RatsnestGraphicsItem):
                self.header_label.setText(f"Связь #{obj.net_code}")
                self._build_ratsnest_ui(obj)
                self.pins_label.hide()
                self.pins_table.hide()
                self.buttons_widget.hide()
            else:
                self.clear_panel()
                return
            self.header_label.show()
            self.form_widget.show()
            self.line_layers_widget.hide()
            self.module_picker_widget.hide()
            self.main_layout.invalidate()
        finally:
            self.setUpdatesEnabled(True)
            self.updateGeometry()
            self.update()

    def clear_panel(self):
        self.current_object = None
        self._set_mode(self.MODE_OBJECT)
        self.header_label.hide()
        self.form_widget.hide()
        self.pins_label.hide()
        self.pins_table.hide()
        self.buttons_widget.hide()
        self.line_layers_widget.hide()
        self.module_picker_widget.hide()
        self.header_label.setText("")
        self.pins_table.setRowCount(0)
        while self.form_layout.rowCount() > 0:
            self.form_layout.removeRow(0)
        self._widgets.clear()

    def _set_mode(self, mode):
        self._mode = mode
        if mode == self.MODE_OBJECT:
            self.line_layers_widget.hide()
            self.module_picker_widget.hide()
        elif mode == self.MODE_LINE_LAYER:
            self.module_picker_widget.hide()
        elif mode == self.MODE_MODULE_PICKER:
            self.line_layers_widget.hide()

    def _on_layer_clicked(self, item):
        name = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if name:
            self.line_layer_selected.emit(name)

    def _on_module_tree_clicked(self, item, column):
        data = item.data(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if not data:
            return
        kind, payload = data
        if kind == "module" and payload is not None:
            self.module_selected.emit(payload)

    def refresh_values(self):
        obj = self.current_object
        if not obj or self._building or self._mode != self.MODE_OBJECT:
            return
        self.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        try:
            if isinstance(obj, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                m = obj.module
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(m.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(m.y)
                self._widgets["angle"].setValue(m.angle)
                self._widgets["name"].setText(m.reference)
                self._widgets["value"].setText(m.value)
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardLineGraphicsItem):
                l = obj.model
                self._widgets["x1"].setValue(l.x1)
                self._widgets["y1"].setValue(l.y1)
                self._widgets["x2"].setValue(l.x2)
                self._widgets["y2"].setValue(l.y2)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(l.width)
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardCircleGraphicsItem):
                c = obj.model
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(c.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(c.y)
                self._widgets["radius"].setValue(c.radius)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(c.width)
            elif isinstance(obj, BoardArcGraphicsItem):
                a = obj.model
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(a.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(a.y)
                self._widgets["sx"].setValue(a.sx)
                self._widgets["sy"].setValue(a.sy)
                self._widgets["angle"].setValue(a.angle)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(a.width)
        finally:
            self.setUpdatesEnabled(True)

    def _make_label(self, text):
        label = QLabel(text)
        label.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #a9b7c6;")
        label.setMinimumWidth(90)
        return label

    def _add_row(self, label_text, field_widget, key):
        self.form_layout.addRow(self._make_label(label_text), field_widget)
        self._widgets[key] = field_widget

    def _add_spinbox(self, label, value, min_val, max_val, key, step=0.01, decimals=4, readonly=False):
        le = NumericLineEdit(value, decimals, min_val, max_val)
        le.setReadOnly(readonly)
        self._add_row(label, le, key)

    def _make_combo(self, items, current_index=0):
        combo = QComboBox()
        for label, data in items:
            combo.addItem(label, data)
        combo.setCurrentIndex(current_index)
        view = QListView()
        view.setStyleSheet(COMBO_VIEW_QSS)
        combo.setView(view)
        combo.setMinimumWidth(100)
        combo.setMinimumHeight(24)
        combo.setStyleSheet(inline_combo_qss())
        return combo

    def _line_layer_combo(self, layer):
        choices = [(_layer_label(n), n) for n, _ in DRAWABLE_LAYERS]
        idx = 0
        for i, (_, data) in enumerate(choices):
            if data == layer:
                idx = i
                break
        return self._make_combo(choices, idx)

    def _build_module_ui(self, module):
        self._add_spinbox("X, мм:", module.x, -100000.0, 100000.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Y, мм:", module.y, -100000.0, 100000.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Угол, °:", module.angle, -359.0, 359.0, "angle", decimals=1)
        name_input = QLineEdit(module.reference)
        name_input.setMinimumWidth(100)
        name_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Имя:", name_input, "name")
        val_input = QLineEdit(module.value)
        val_input.setMinimumWidth(100)
        val_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Значение:", val_input, "value")
        self._add_row("Слой:", self._make_combo(
            [("F.Cu", "F.Cu"), ("B.Cu", "B.Cu")],
            0 if module.layer == "F.Cu" else 1,
        ), "layer")
        fp_input = QLineEdit(module.footprint.name)
        fp_input.setReadOnly(True)
        fp_input.setMinimumWidth(100)
        fp_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Корпус:", fp_input, "footprint")
        self._fill_pins_table(module)

    def _fill_pins_table(self, module):
        nets = module.pad_nets
        self.pins_table.setRowCount(0)
        for pad in module.footprint.pads:
            row = self.pins_table.rowCount()
            self.pins_table.insertRow(row)
            self.pins_table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(f"{module.reference}.{pad.number}"))
            pad_net = nets.get(pad.number)
            self.pins_table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(pad_net.net_name if pad_net else ""))
            self.pins_table.setItem(row, 2, QTableWidgetItem(str(pad_net.net_code) if pad_net else ""))

    def _build_line_ui(self, line):
        self._add_spinbox("X1, мм:", line.x1, -100000.0, 100000.0, "x1")
        self._add_spinbox("Y1, мм:", line.y1, -100000.0, 100000.0, "y1")
        self._add_spinbox("X2, мм:", line.x2, -100000.0, 100000.0, "x2")
        self._add_spinbox("Y2, мм:", line.y2, -100000.0, 100000.0, "y2")
        self._add_spinbox("Толщина, мм:", line.width, 0.01, 5.0, "width")
        self._add_row("Слой:", self._line_layer_combo(line.layer), "layer")

    def _build_circle_ui(self, circle):
        self._add_spinbox("Центр X, мм:", circle.x, -100000.0, 100000.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Центр Y, мм:", circle.y, -100000.0, 100000.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Радиус, мм:", circle.radius, 0.01, 10000.0, "radius")
        self._add_spinbox("Толщина, мм:", circle.width, 0.01, 5.0, "width")
        self._add_row("Слой:", self._line_layer_combo(circle.layer), "layer")

    def _build_arc_ui(self, arc):
        self._add_spinbox("Центр X, мм:", arc.x, -100000.0, 100000.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Центр Y, мм:", arc.y, -100000.0, 100000.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Начало X, мм:", arc.sx, -100000.0, 100000.0, "sx")
        self._add_spinbox("Начало Y, мм:", arc.sy, -100000.0, 100000.0, "sy")
        self._add_spinbox("Угол, °:", arc.angle, -359.0, 359.0, "angle")
        self._add_spinbox("Толщина, мм:", arc.width, 0.01, 5.0, "width")
        self._add_row("Слой:", self._line_layer_combo(arc.layer), "layer")

    def _build_text_ui(self, text):
        self._add_spinbox("X, мм:", text.x, -100000.0, 100000.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Y, мм:", text.y, -100000.0, 100000.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Размер, мм:", text.size, 0.05, 50.0, "size")
        text_input = QLineEdit(text.text)
        text_input.setMinimumWidth(100)
        text_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Текст:", text_input, "text")
        visible = QCheckBox(); visible.setChecked(text.visible); visible.setStyleSheet(CHECKBOX_QSS)
        self._add_row("Видимый:", visible, "visible")
        self._add_row("Слой:", self._line_layer_combo(text.layer), "layer")

    def _build_ratsnest_ui(self, item):
        code_input = QLineEdit(str(item.net_code)); code_input.setReadOnly(True)
        code_input.setMinimumWidth(100); code_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Код цепи:", code_input, "net_code")
        name_input = QLineEdit(item.net_name); name_input.setReadOnly(True)
        name_input.setMinimumWidth(100); name_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Имя цепи:", name_input, "net_name")
        a_input = QLineEdit(item.label_a); a_input.setReadOnly(True)
        a_input.setMinimumWidth(100); a_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Пин A:", a_input, "label_a")
        b_input = QLineEdit(item.label_b); b_input.setReadOnly(True)
        b_input.setMinimumWidth(100); b_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Пин B:", b_input, "label_b")
        if item.is_gnd:
            power_text = "GND"
        elif item.is_power:
            power_text = "Питание"
        else:
            power_text = "Нет"
        p_input = QLineEdit(power_text); p_input.setReadOnly(True)
        p_input.setMinimumWidth(100); p_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Тип цепи:", p_input, "is_power")

    def _reset_changes(self):
        if self.current_object is not None:
            self.show_object(self.current_object)

    def _apply_changes(self):
        obj = self.current_object
        if not obj:
            return
        if isinstance(obj, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
            m = obj.module
            m.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
            m.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
            m.angle = self._widgets["angle"].value()
            m.reference = self._widgets["name"].text()
            m.value = self._widgets["value"].text()
            m.layer = self._widgets["layer"].currentData()
            obj.setPos(m.x, m.y)
            obj.setRotation(m.angle)
            obj.prepareGeometryChange()
            obj.update()
            if hasattr(obj.canvas, 'ratsnest_update_requested'):
                obj.canvas.ratsnest_update_requested.emit()
        elif isinstance(obj, BoardLineGraphicsItem):
            l = obj.model
            l.x1 = self._widgets["x1"].value()
            l.y1 = self._widgets["y1"].value()
            l.x2 = self._widgets["x2"].value()
            l.y2 = self._widgets["y2"].value()
            l.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
            l.layer = self._widgets["layer"].currentData()
            obj.setPos(l.x1, l.y1)
            obj.prepareGeometryChange(); obj.update()
        elif isinstance(obj, BoardCircleGraphicsItem):
            c = obj.model
            c.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
            c.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
            c.radius = self._widgets["radius"].value()
            c.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
            c.layer = self._widgets["layer"].currentData()
            obj.setPos(c.x, c.y)
            obj.prepareGeometryChange(); obj.update()
        elif isinstance(obj, BoardArcGraphicsItem):
            a = obj.model
            a.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
            a.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
            a.sx = self._widgets["sx"].value()
            a.sy = self._widgets["sy"].value()
            a.angle = self._widgets["angle"].value()
            a.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
            a.layer = self._widgets["layer"].currentData()
            obj.setPos(a.x, a.y)
            obj.prepareGeometryChange(); obj.update()
        elif isinstance(obj, BoardTextGraphicsItem):
            t = obj.model
            t.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
            t.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
            t.size = self._widgets["size"].value()
            t.text = self._widgets["text"].text() or "Text"
            t.visible = self._widgets["visible"].isChecked()
            t.layer = self._widgets["layer"].currentData()
            obj.setPos(t.x, t.y)
            obj.prepareGeometryChange(); obj.update().
.

        self.properties_panel.apply_theme().

from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton
from PySide6.QtGui import QIcon
from PySide6.QtCore import Signal, Qt
from constants import Styles


class BoardTool:
    SELECT = "select"
    ADD_LINE = "add_line"
    ADD_CIRCLE = "add_circle"
    ADD_ARC = "add_arc"
    ADD_TEXT = "add_text"
    ADD_MODULE = "add_module"
    ADD_NET = "add_net"


TOOL_BUTTONS = [
    (BoardTool.ADD_LINE, "╱", "Линия (1)"),
    (BoardTool.ADD_CIRCLE, "◯", "Окружность (2)"),

def _load_icon(name):
    path = ICONS_DIR / f"{name}.svg"
    if path.exists():
        return QIcon(str(path))
    return QIcon()


class BoardDrawingToolBar(QWidget):
    tool_selected = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(8, 6, 8, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        self.buttons = {}
        self._active = None

        for tool, fallback, tip in TOOL_BUTTONS:
            btn = QPushButton(fallback)
            btn.setFixedSize(BUTTON_SIZE, BUTTON_SIZE)
            btn.setToolTip(tip)
            btn.setCheckable(True)
            btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _=False, t=tool: self._on_clicked(t))
            layout.addWidget(btn)
            self.buttons[tool] = btn

        layout.addStretch()
        self.setObjectName("BoardDrawingToolBar")
        self.setStyleSheet(
            "QWidget#BoardDrawingToolBar { background-color: #2b2b2b;"
            " border-top: 1px solid #1e1e1e; }"
        )
        self._update_styles()

    def _on_clicked(self, tool):
        self.set_active(tool)
        self.tool_selected.emit(tool)

    def set_active(self, tool):
        self._active = tool
        self._update_styles()

    def clear_active(self):
        self._active = None
        self._update_styles()

    def _update_styles(self):
        for tool, btn in self.buttons.items():
            is_active = (self._active == tool)
            btn.setChecked(is_active)
            btn.setStyleSheet(Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_ACTIVE if is_active
                              else Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_INACTIVE)

    def apply_theme(self):
        self._update_styles().
.

FILE: D:\Desktop\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\ui\window\board_files.py

from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFileDialog, QMessageBox

from pbe_editor.core.board_models import Board, BoardModule, PadNet
from pbe_editor.core.netlist_parser import parse_netlist
from kicad.brd_exporter import build_brd_text
from kicad.brd_importer import parse_brd


class BoardFilesService:
    def on_import_net(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Открыть сетлист", "",
                                              "Netlist (*.net);;All Files (*)")
        if not path:
            return
        self.refresh_library_modules()
        try:
            components = parse_netlist(path)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось разобрать сетлист: {e}")
            return

        self.manager.clear()
        self.board = Board(name=Path(path).stem)
        self.manager.board = self.board

        missing = []
        next_uid = 1
        for comp in components:
            fp = self.find_footprint(comp.footprint)
            if fp is None:
                missing.append(f"{comp.reference} ({comp.footprint})")
                continue
            module = BoardModule(
                footprint=fp, reference=comp.reference, value=comp.value,
                x=0.0, y=0.0, angle=0.0, layer="F.Cu", uid=next_uid,
            )
            next_uid += 1
            pad_numbers = {p.number for p in fp.pads}
            for pad_number, net_name in comp.pins.items():
                if pad_number not in pad_numbers or not net_name:
                    continue
                net = self.board.get_or_create_net(net_name)
                module.pad_nets[pad_number] = PadNet(
                    pad_number=pad_number,
                    net_code=net.code,
                    net_name=net.name,
                )
            self.board.modules.append(module)
            self.manager.add_module(module)
        self.refresh_module_list()
        self.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.show_log(f"Импортировано модулей: {len(self.board.modules)}")
        self._reset_history()
        self._modified = True
        if missing:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Импорт завершён",
                "Импорт выполнен, но не найдены посадочные места:\n" + "\n".join(missing))

    def on_open_board(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Открыть плату", "",
                                              "KiCad Board (*.brd);;All Files (*)")
        if not path:
            return
        self.load_board_from_path(path)

    def load_board_from_path(self, path):
        try:
            board = parse_brd(path)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось открыть плату: {e}")
            return
        self.manager.clear()
        self.board = board
        self.manager.board = self.board
        self.board.name = Path(path).stem

        for module in self.board.modules:
            self.manager.add_module(module)
        for line in self.board.lines:
            self.manager.add_line(line)
        for circle in self.board.circles:
            self.manager.add_circle(circle)
        for arc in self.board.arcs:
            self.manager.add_arc(arc)
        for text in self.board.texts:
            self.manager.add_text(text)

        self.refresh_module_list()
        self.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.board.filename = path
        self.show_log(f"Плата загружена: {Path(path).name} ({len(self.board.modules)} модулей)")
        self._reset_history()
        self._modified = False

    def save_board(self):
        if not self.board.filename:
            self.on_save_board_as()
            return
        try:
            text = build_brd_text(self.board)
            Path(self.board.filename).write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
            self._modified = False
            self.show_log(f"Плата сохранена: {Path(self.board.filename).name}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось сохранить: {e}")

    def on_save_board_as(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Сохранить плату как", "",
                                              "KiCad Board (*.brd);;All Files (*)")
        if not path:
            return
        if not path.lower().endswith('.brd'):
            path += '.brd'
        try:
            text = build_brd_text(self.board)
            Path(path).write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
            self.board.filename = path
            self.board.name = Path(path).stem
            self._modified = False
            self.show_log(f"Плата сохранена: {Path(path).name}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось сохранить: {e}").
.

class BoardStateService:
    def next_module_uid(self):
        if not self.board.modules:
            return 1
        return max(m.uid for m in self.board.modules) + 1

    def next_line_uid(self):
        if not self.board.lines:
            return 1
        return max(l.uid for l in self.board.lines) + 1

    def next_circle_uid(self):
        if not self.board.circles:
            return 1
        return max(c.uid for c in self.board.circles) + 1

    def next_arc_uid(self):
        if not self.board.arcs:
            return 1
        return max(a.uid for a in self.board.arcs) + 1

    def next_text_uid(self):
        if not self.board.texts:
            return 1
        return max(t.uid for t in self.board.texts) + 1.
.

from constants import GRID_SERIES, GRID_SERIES_LABEL


SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_GRID_MODE = "brd_grid_mode"


class GridService:
    def _load_grid_mode(self):
        mode = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_GRID_MODE, "1x", type=str)
        return mode if mode in GRID_SERIES else "1x"

    def _persist_grid_mode(self, mode):
        self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_GRID_MODE, mode)
        self._settings.sync()

    def apply_grid_series(self, mode, initial=False):
        if mode not in GRID_SERIES:
            return
        self.grid_mode = mode
        self._persist_grid_mode(mode)
        for m, act in self._grid_actions.items():
            act.setChecked(m == mode)
        idx = self.grid_series_combo.findData(mode)
        if idx >= 0 and self.grid_series_combo.currentIndex() != idx:
            self.grid_series_combo.blockSignals(True)
            self.grid_series_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
            self.grid_series_combo.blockSignals(False)
        values = GRID_SERIES[mode]
        self.grid_combo.blockSignals(True)
        self.grid_combo.clear()
        self.grid_combo.addItems(values)
        default = "1.000" if mode == "1x" else "1.270"
        self.grid_combo.setCurrentText(default if default in values else values[0])
        self.grid_combo.blockSignals(False)
        self.canvas.set_grid_size(float(self.grid_combo.currentText()))
        if not initial:
            self.show_log(f"Ряд сетки: {GRID_SERIES_LABEL[mode]}")

    def toggle_grid_series(self):
        self.apply_grid_series("1.27x" if self.grid_mode == "1x" else "1x")

    def toggle_grid(self):
        self.canvas.show_grid = self.act_show_grid.isChecked()
        self.canvas.viewport().update()

    def on_grid_changed(self, text):
        if not text:
            return
        self.canvas.set_grid_size(float(text))
        self.canvas.setFocus()

    def change_grid_step(self, direction):
        i = self.grid_combo.currentIndex() + direction
        if 0 <= i < self.grid_combo.count():
            self.grid_combo.setCurrentIndex(i)

    def update_zoom_label(self, zoom):
        self.zoom_label.setText(f" {int(zoom * 100)}% ").
.

import copy


class HistoryService:
    def snapshot(self):
        return {
            "modules": copy.deepcopy(self.board.modules),
            "lines": copy.deepcopy(self.board.lines),
            "circles": copy.deepcopy(self.board.circles),
            "arcs": copy.deepcopy(self.board.arcs),
            "texts": copy.deepcopy(self.board.texts),
            "nets": copy.deepcopy(self.board.nets),
        }

    def restore_snapshot(self, snap):
        self.board.modules = copy.deepcopy(snap["modules"])
        self.board.lines = copy.deepcopy(snap["lines"])
        self.board.circles = copy.deepcopy(snap["circles"])
        self.board.arcs = copy.deepcopy(snap["arcs"])
        self.board.texts = copy.deepcopy(snap.get("texts", []))
        self.board.nets = copy.deepcopy(snap["nets"])
        self.manager.clear()
        self.manager.board = self.board
        for m in self.board.modules:
            self.manager.add_module(m)
        for l in self.board.lines:
            self.manager.add_line(l)
        for c in self.board.circles:
            self.manager.add_circle(c)
        for a in self.board.arcs:
            self.manager.add_arc(a)
        for t in self.board.texts:
            self.manager.add_text(t)
        if self.current_tool != "select":
            self._set_selectable_all(False)
        self.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()
        self.refresh_module_list()

    def _reset_history(self):
        self._undo_stack = []
        self._redo_stack = []
        self._current_snapshot = self.snapshot()
        self._update_history_buttons()

    def commit_snapshot(self):
        self._modified = True
        if self._current_snapshot is None:
            self._current_snapshot = self.snapshot()
            return
        self._undo_stack.append(self._current_snapshot)
        if len(self._undo_stack) > 100:
            self._undo_stack.pop(0)
        self._redo_stack.clear()
        self._current_snapshot = self.snapshot()
        self._update_history_buttons()

    def undo(self):
        if not self._undo_stack:
            return
        self._redo_stack.append(self._current_snapshot)
        self._current_snapshot = self._undo_stack.pop()
        self.restore_snapshot(self._current_snapshot)
        self._update_history_buttons()
        self._modified = True
        self.show_log("отменено")

    def redo(self):
        if not self._redo_stack:
            return
        self._undo_stack.append(self._current_snapshot)
        self._current_snapshot = self._redo_stack.pop()
        self.restore_snapshot(self._current_snapshot)
        self._update_history_buttons()
        self._modified = True
        self.show_log("повторено")

    def _update_history_buttons(self):
        self.aux_bar.set_undo_enabled(bool(self._undo_stack))
        self.aux_bar.set_redo_enabled(bool(self._redo_stack)).
.

from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFileDialog, QDialog

from constants.board_settings import SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LIBS
from common.ui.dialogs.libraries_dialog import LibrariesManagerDialog
from kicad.mod_importer import parse_kicad_mod
from pbe_editor.ui.panels.drawing_tool_bar import BoardTool


class LibrariesService:
    def load_library_paths(self):
        saved = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LIBS, "", type=str)
        if saved:
            self.library_paths = [p for p in saved.split("\n") if p.strip()]

    def save_library_paths(self):
        self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LIBS, "\n".join(self.library_paths))
        self._settings.sync()

    def refresh_library_modules(self):
        self.library_modules = {}
        for path in self.library_paths:
            try:
                footprints = parse_kicad_mod(path)
                self.library_modules[path] = {fp.name: fp for fp in footprints}
            except Exception as e:
                self.show_log(f"Ошибка библиотеки {Path(path).name}: {e}")

    def find_footprint(self, name):
        for lib_data in self.library_modules.values():
            if name in lib_data:
                return lib_data[name]
        return None

    def add_library(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Добавить библиотеку .mod", "",
                                              "KiCad Library (*.mod);;All Files (*)")
        if not path or path in self.library_paths:
            return
        self.library_paths.append(path)
        self.save_library_paths()
        self.refresh_library_modules()
        if self.current_tool == BoardTool.ADD_MODULE:
            self.right_panel.properties_panel.show_module_picker(self.library_modules)
        self.show_log(f"Библиотека добавлена: {Path(path).name}")

    def manage_libraries(self):
        dlg = LibrariesManagerDialog(self.library_paths, self)
        if dlg.exec() != QDialog.Accepted:
            return
        new_paths = dlg.get_library_paths()
        if new_paths == self.library_paths:
            return
        self.library_paths = new_paths
        self.save_library_paths()
        self.refresh_library_modules()
        if self.current_tool == BoardTool.ADD_MODULE:
            self.right_panel.properties_panel.show_module_picker(self.library_modules)
        self.show_log(f"Библиотек: {len(self.library_paths)}").
.

from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import QSettings

from constants import SETTINGS_ORG, SETTINGS_APP
from constants.board_settings import SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LAST
from common.ui.dialogs.custom_question_dialog import CustomQuestionDialog


class SessionService:
    def _init_session(self):
        self._settings = QSettings(SETTINGS_ORG, SETTINGS_APP)

    def save_last_board(self):
        if self.board.filename:
            self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LAST, self.board.filename)
        else:
            self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LAST, "")
        self._settings.sync()

    def _restore_last_board(self):
        last = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_BRD_LAST, "", type=str)
        if not last:
            self._reset_history()
            return
        p = Path(last)
        if not p.exists() or not p.is_file():
            self._reset_history()
            return
        self.load_board_from_path(str(p))

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if self._modified:
            dlg = CustomQuestionDialog(
                self,
                title="Несохранённые изменения",
                message=f"Сохранить изменения в плате '{self.board.name}' перед выходом?"
            )
            result = dlg.exec()
            if result == CustomQuestionDialog.ResultSave:
                self.save_board()
                if self._modified:
                    event.ignore()
                    return
            elif result == CustomQuestionDialog.ResultCancel:
                event.ignore()
                return
        self.save_last_board()
        super().closeEvent(event).
.

from PySide6.QtCore import QEvent, Qt

from constants import (
    PAN_KEYS,
    VK_ESCAPE, VK_DELETE, VK_F1, VK_F2, VK_F3, VK_F11, VK_G,
    VK_R, VK_L, VK_O,
)


class KeyboardService:
    def handle_key_press(self, event):
        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Escape:
            self.exit_to_select_mode()
            return True
        vk = event.nativeVirtualKey()
        if vk == VK_O:
            self.act_show_values.setChecked(not self.act_show_values.isChecked())
            self.toggle_values()
            return True
        if vk == VK_DELETE:
            self.commands.delete_selected()
            return True
        if vk == VK_R:
            self.commands.rotate_selected()
            return True
        if vk == VK_L:
            self.act_show_ratsnest.setChecked(not self.act_show_ratsnest.isChecked())
            self.on_toggle_ratsnest()
            return True
        if vk == VK_G:
            self.act_show_grid.setChecked(not self.act_show_grid.isChecked())
            self.toggle_grid()
            return True
        if vk == VK_F11:
            self.toggle_fullscreen()
            return True
        if vk == VK_F1:
            self.change_grid_step(-1)
            return True
        if vk == VK_F2:
            self.change_grid_step(1)
            return True
        if vk == VK_F3:
            self.toggle_grid_series()
            return True
        if vk in PAN_KEYS:
            self.canvas.pressed_keys.add(vk)
            if not self.canvas.pan_timer.isActive():
                self.canvas.pan_timer.start()
            return True
        return False

    def handle_key_release(self, event):
        vk = event.nativeVirtualKey()
        if vk in PAN_KEYS:
            self.canvas.pressed_keys.discard(vk)
            if not self.canvas.pressed_keys:
                self.canvas.pan_timer.stop()
            return True
        return False

    def eventFilter(self, obj, event):
        if event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyPress:
            if self.handle_key_press(event):
                return True
        elif event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyRelease:
            if self.handle_key_release(event):
                return True
        return super().eventFilter(obj, event).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QGraphicsView

from pbe_editor.ui.panels.drawing_tool_bar import BoardTool
from pbe_editor.graphics.module_item import BoardModuleGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.graphics.board_line_item import (
    BoardLineGraphicsItem, BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
    BoardTextGraphicsItem,
)
from pbe_editor.graphics.ratsnest_item import RatsnestGraphicsItem


class ToolsService:
    def set_selectable_all(self, enabled):
        for item in self.canvas.scene.items():
            if isinstance(item, (BoardModuleGraphicsItem, BoardLineGraphicsItem,
                                 BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
                                 BoardTextGraphicsItem, RatsnestGraphicsItem)):
                try:
                    item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, enabled)
                    item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, enabled)
                except RuntimeError:
                    pass

    def set_tool(self, tool):
        self.current_tool = tool
        self._line_start = None
        self._circle_center_point = None
        self._arc_center_point = None
        self._arc_start_point = None
        self._text_position = None
        self._net_start = None
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_line()
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_circle()
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_arc()
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_text()
        self.canvas.clear_highlight_pad()

        if tool in (BoardTool.SELECT, BoardTool.ADD_NET):
            self.canvas.set_show_snap(False)
        else:
            self.canvas.set_show_snap(True)

        if tool == BoardTool.SELECT:
            self.set_selectable_all(True)
            self.aux_bar.set_select_active(True)
            self.tool_bar.clear_active()
            self.canvas.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag)
            self.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()
        else:
            self.set_selectable_all(False)
            self.canvas.scene.clearSelection()
            self.aux_bar.set_select_active(False)
            self.tool_bar.set_active(tool)
            self.canvas.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.NoDrag)
            if tool in (BoardTool.ADD_LINE, BoardTool.ADD_CIRCLE,
                        BoardTool.ADD_ARC, BoardTool.ADD_TEXT):
                self.right_panel.properties_panel.show_line_layer_picker(self._line_layer)
            elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_MODULE:
                self.right_panel.properties_panel.show_module_picker(self.library_modules)
            elif tool == BoardTool.ADD_NET:
                self.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()
        self.show_log(f"Инструмент: {tool}")

    def exit_to_select_mode(self):
        self.set_tool(BoardTool.SELECT)
        self.canvas.scene.clearSelection().
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                               QListWidget, QComboBox, QToolBar, QStatusBar,

class UiSetupService:
    def init_canvas(self):
        self.canvas = BoardCanvas(self)
        self.canvas.zoom_changed.connect(self.update_zoom_label)
        self.canvas.mouse_moved.connect(self.commands.on_mouse_moved)
        self.canvas.ratsnest_update_requested.connect(self.on_ratsnest_update)
        self.canvas.canvas_clicked.connect(self.commands.on_canvas_clicked)
        self.canvas.canvas_right_clicked.connect(self.commands.on_canvas_right_clicked)
        self.canvas.selection_changed.connect(self.commands.on_selection_changed)
        self.canvas.content_changed.connect(self.commit_snapshot)
        self.manager = BoardManager(self.board, self.canvas.scene, self.canvas)

        self.aux_bar = AuxBar(object_name="BoardAuxBar")
        self.aux_bar.undo_requested.connect(self.undo)
        self.aux_bar.redo_requested.connect(self.redo)
        self.aux_bar.select_requested.connect(lambda: self.set_tool(BoardTool.SELECT))
        self.aux_bar.delete_requested.connect(self.commands.delete_selected)

        self.tool_bar = BoardDrawingToolBar()
        self.tool_bar.tool_selected.connect(self.set_tool)

        central = QWidget()
        v = QVBoxLayout(central)
        v.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        v.setSpacing(0)
        v.addWidget(self.aux_bar, 0)
        v.addWidget(self.canvas, 1)
        v.addWidget(self.tool_bar, 0)
        self.setCentralWidget(central)

    def init_toolbars(self):
        toolbar = QToolBar("Верхняя панель", self)
        toolbar.setMovable(False)
        self.addToolBar(Qt.ToolBarArea.TopToolBarArea, toolbar)
        self.toolbar = toolbar

        toolbar.addWidget(QLabel("Сетка: "))
        self.grid_series_combo = QComboBox()
        for mode, label in GRID_SERIES_LABEL.items():
            self.grid_series_combo.addItem(label, mode)
        idx = self.grid_series_combo.findData(self.grid_mode)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.grid_series_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.grid_series_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(
            lambda i: self.apply_grid_series(self.grid_series_combo.itemData(i))
        )
        toolbar.addWidget(self.grid_series_combo)

        toolbar.addWidget(QLabel(" Шаг: "))
        self.grid_combo = QComboBox()
        self.grid_combo.currentTextChanged.connect(self.on_grid_changed)
        toolbar.addWidget(self.grid_combo)

        toolbar.addSeparator()
        self.zoom_label = QLabel(" 100% ")
        toolbar.addWidget(self.zoom_label)

    def init_menu(self):
        menubar = QMenuBar(self)
        menubar.setNativeMenuBar(False)
        self.setMenuBar(menubar)

        file_menu = menubar.addMenu("Файл")
        act_import_net = QAction("Импорт схемы (.net)...", self)
        act_import_net.triggered.connect(self.on_import_net)
        file_menu.addAction(act_import_net)

        act_open = QAction("Открыть плату (.brd)...", self)
        act_open.triggered.connect(self.on_open_board)
        file_menu.addAction(act_open)

        act_save = QAction("Сохранить", self)
        act_save.setShortcut("Ctrl+S")
        act_save.triggered.connect(self.save_board)
        file_menu.addAction(act_save)

        act_save_as = QAction("Сохранить как...", self)
        act_save_as.setShortcut("Ctrl+Shift+S")
        act_save_as.triggered.connect(self.on_save_board_as)
        file_menu.addAction(act_save_as)

        file_menu.addSeparator()

        act_add_lib = QAction("Добавить библиотеку...", self)
        act_add_lib.triggered.connect(self.add_library)
        file_menu.addAction(act_add_lib)

        act_manage_lib = QAction("Подключенные библиотеки...", self)
        act_manage_lib.triggered.connect(self.manage_libraries)
        file_menu.addAction(act_manage_lib)

        file_menu.addSeparator()
        act_exit = QAction("Выход", self)
        act_exit.setShortcut("Ctrl+Q")
        act_exit.triggered.connect(self.close)
        file_menu.addAction(act_exit)

        edit_menu = menubar.addMenu("Правка")
        act_undo = QAction("Отменить", self)
        act_undo.setShortcut("Ctrl+Z")
        act_undo.triggered.connect(self.undo)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_undo)
        act_redo = QAction("Повторить", self)
        act_redo.setShortcut("Ctrl+Shift+Z")
        act_redo.triggered.connect(self.redo)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_redo)

        edit_menu.addSeparator()

        act_copy = QAction("Копировать", self)
        act_copy.setShortcut("Ctrl+C")
        act_copy.triggered.connect(self.commands.copy_selected)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_copy)

        act_paste = QAction("Вставить", self)
        act_paste.setShortcut("Ctrl+V")
        act_paste.triggered.connect(self.commands.paste)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_paste)

        act_delete = QAction("Удалить", self)
        act_delete.setShortcut("Del")
        act_delete.triggered.connect(self.commands.delete_selected)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_delete)

        operations_menu = menubar.addMenu("Операции")
        
        act_autoplace = QAction("Авторазмещение...", self)
        act_autoplace.setShortcut("Ctrl+P")
        act_autoplace.triggered.connect(self.commands.on_autoplace)
        operations_menu.addAction(act_autoplace)
        
        act_reset = QAction("Сбросить размещение", self)
        act_reset.setShortcut("Ctrl+R")
        act_reset.triggered.connect(self.commands.on_reset_placement)
        operations_menu.addAction(act_reset)

        view_menu = menubar.addMenu("Вид")
        self.act_show_ratsnest = QAction("Показать связи", self, checkable=True)
        self.act_show_ratsnest.setChecked(True)
        self.act_show_ratsnest.setShortcut("L")
        self.act_show_ratsnest.triggered.connect(self.on_toggle_ratsnest)
        view_menu.addAction(self.act_show_ratsnest)

        self.act_show_values = QAction("Показать значения", self, checkable=True)
        self.act_show_values.setChecked(self.canvas.show_values)
        self.act_show_values.setShortcut("O")
        self.act_show_values.triggered.connect(self.toggle_values)
        view_menu.addAction(self.act_show_values)

        act_nets = QAction("Список связей...", self)
        act_nets.triggered.connect(self.on_show_nets)
        view_menu.addAction(act_nets)
        
        view_menu.addSeparator()

        self.act_show_grid = QAction("Показывать сетку", self, checkable=True)
        self.act_show_grid.setChecked(True)
        self.act_show_grid.setShortcut("G")
        self.act_show_grid.triggered.connect(self.toggle_grid)
        view_menu.addAction(self.act_show_grid)

        grid_menu = view_menu.addMenu("Шаг сетки")
        self._grid_group = QActionGroup(self)
        self._grid_group.setExclusive(True)
        self._grid_actions = {}
        for mode, label in GRID_SERIES_LABEL.items():
            act = QAction(label, self, checkable=True)
            act.setChecked(mode == self.grid_mode)
            act.triggered.connect(lambda _=False, m=mode: self.apply_grid_series(m))
            self._grid_group.addAction(act)
            grid_menu.addAction(act)
            self._grid_actions[mode] = act

        view_menu.addSeparator()
        act_full = QAction("Полный экран", self)
        act_full.setShortcut("F11")
        act_full.triggered.connect(self.toggle_fullscreen)
        view_menu.addAction(act_full)

    def init_docks(self):
        self.module_dock = QDockWidget("", self)
        self.module_dock.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)

        panel = QWidget()
        panel.setMinimumWidth(160)
        panel.setMaximumWidth(320)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(panel)
        layout.setContentsMargins(6, 6, 6, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        header = QHBoxLayout()
        header.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        title = QLabel("Модули на плате")
        title.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        header.addWidget(title)
        header.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(header)

        self.module_list = QListWidget()
        self.module_list.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.module_list.itemClicked.connect(self.commands.on_module_list_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.module_list, 1)

        self.module_dock.setWidget(panel)
        self.addDockWidget(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea, self.module_dock)
        self._hide_dock_title(self.module_dock)

        self.properties_dock = QDockWidget("", self)
        self.properties_dock.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)
        self.right_panel = BoardRightPanel()
        self.properties_dock.setWidget(self.right_panel)
        self.addDockWidget(Qt.RightDockWidgetArea, self.properties_dock)
        self._hide_dock_title(self.properties_dock)

    def _hide_dock_title(self, dock):
        empty = QWidget()
        empty.setFixedHeight(0)
        dock.setTitleBarWidget(empty)

    def init_statusbar(self):
        sb = QStatusBar()
        self.setStatusBar(sb)
        self.log_label = QLabel("Готово.")
        sb.addWidget(self.log_label)
        self.coords_label = QLabel("X: 0.0000, Y: 0.0000")
        sb.addPermanentWidget(self.coords_label)

    def apply_theme(self):
        app = QApplication.instance()
        app.setStyleSheet(Styles.TOOLTIP)
        self.setStyleSheet(Styles.MAIN_WINDOW)
        self.canvas.apply_theme()
        self.log_label.setStyleSheet(Styles.LOG_LABEL)
        self.right_panel.setStyleSheet(Styles.RIGHT_PANEL)
        self.right_panel.apply_theme()
        self.tool_bar.apply_theme()
        self.aux_bar.apply_theme()
        self.module_dock.setStyleSheet(Styles.MAIN_WINDOW)

    def wire_panels(self):
        self.right_panel.properties_panel.btn_apply.clicked.connect(self.commands.on_apply_properties)
        self.right_panel.properties_panel.line_layer_selected.connect(self.commands.on_line_layer_selected)
        self.right_panel.properties_panel.module_selected.connect(self.commands.on_module_picked_from_panel)

    def toggle_fullscreen(self):
        if self.isFullScreen():
            self.showNormal()
            self.setWindowState(self.windowState() | Qt.WindowState.WindowMaximized)
        else:
            self.showFullScreen()

    def on_show_nets(self):
        dlg = NetsDialog(self.board, self)
        dlg.exec()

    def on_toggle_ratsnest(self):
        self.manager.show_ratsnest = self.act_show_ratsnest.isChecked()
        self.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()

    def on_ratsnest_update(self):
        self.manager.rebuild_ratsnest()

    def _apply_startup_geometry(self):
        screen = QGuiApplication.primaryScreen()
        if screen is None:
            self.resize(1280, 800)
            return
        avail = screen.availableGeometry()
        self.setGeometry(avail)
        self.setWindowState(self.windowState() | Qt.WindowState.WindowMaximized)

    def toggle_values(self):
        self.canvas.set_show_values(self.act_show_values.isChecked())
        state = "включены" if self.canvas.show_values else "скрыты"
        self.show_log(f"Значения элементов {state}").
.
